Result: 在群体动力学问题和工业CFD应用（预测不可压缩约束下的雷诺应力张量分量）上验证了所提出框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够在保持物理约束的前提下，对称地联合建模多个高维物理场，克服了传统推导法对输出选择敏感的问题，并在实际应用中展现出良好性能。

Abstract: We address the simultaneous prediction of multiple high-dimensional physical fields governed by linear equality constraints, a setting that arises in many real-world applications in physics machine learning. Gaussian process (GP) regression is a widely used surrogate modeling approach due to its effectiveness in small-sample regimes and its ability to provide uncertainty quantification. However, applying GP models in this setting raises two major challenges: the high dimensionality of the discretized output fields and the enforcement of the physical constraint in predictions. For the latter, a common strategy consists in deducing one output from the others via the constraint relation. Through a benchmark, we show that this deductive approach is sensitive to the arbitrary choice of which output to deduce, affecting both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification. Consequently, there is a need for an approach that treats all fields symmetrically while strictly respecting the underlying physics. Motivated by these limitations, we propose a robust framework for jointly modeling constrained multi-field data. Our approach first leverages a specific PCA procedure for multi-field data, coined row-wise PCA, which has the interesting property of preserving the constraint in the latent space. Since standard PCA strategies for multi-field data do not preserve such constraints, we investigate theoretically the optimality of the row-wise choice. In a second step, we consider a linearly-constrained multi-output GP approach based on a specific kernel parametrization which is trained on the latent space of row-wise PCA. The proposed framework is validated on a population dynamics problem and on an industrial CFD application, which involves the prediction of Reynolds stress tensor components under the incompressibility constraint.

</details>

Conclusion: 该降维方法不仅计算高效，而且能够直接估计真实密度的DIM，在独立性成分分析和充分降维等经典问题上具有理论联系，并在聚类分析和异常检测等实际任务中展示出实用价值。

Abstract: An intuitive method for dimensionality reduction is proposed, which is highly effective for finding interesting projections of multivariate data. Following similar intuitive motivation to a number of existing techniques, the proposed method is based on enhancing the nearest neighbour relationships in the data. The proposed projection arises from the spectral decomposition of a matrix designed to encode the local covariance structure in the data, where the local covariance at a point is captured by pairs of its nearest neighbours. We show that under standard regularity conditions this matrix is a consistent estimator of the so-called ``Density Information Matrix'' (DIM); a non-parametric analogue of the Fisher Information Matrix. Spectral decompositions of DIMs have been shown to be connected with the important problems of Independent Components Analysis and, in the supervised context, Sufficient Dimension Reduction. However, existing estimators of the DIM are computationally expensive to compute and only target the DIM of a surrogate density, which is proportional to the square of the true underlying density. In addition, we go on to explore the practical utility of our method in aiding the downstream tasks of cluster analysis and outlier detection.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gromov-Wasserstein通过逐点关系比较分布，在一对多场景（多个目标结果细化一个源状态）中过于敏感，而目标条件分布的均值才承载感兴趣的几何信息。需要一种更鲁棒的比较框架。

Conclusion: 本文的弱GW框架通过均值保持细化机制，有效缓解了一对多场景下传统GW的过度敏感性，同时保留了传输几何的结构信息。理论性质（存在性、对偶形式、收缩收敛）与实验验证共同表明该方法在分布比较和特征细化任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) compares distributions through relations within each space. This pointwise comparison can be too sensitive in one-to-many settings, where several target outcomes refine one source state and their mean carries the geometry of interest. We introduce a weak GW framework that compares source relations with relations between the target conditional laws induced by a coupling. For inner-product relations, we retain the conditional means $m_π(x)=\mathbb{E}_π[Y\mid X=x]$. The resulting barycentric weak inner-product GW (wIGW) satisfies $\mathrm{wIGW}_{\mathrm{bar}}^2(μ,ν)=\inf_{η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν}\mathrm{IGW}^2(μ,η)$. Here $η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν$ means that $ν$ is a mean-preserving spread of $η$. Thus wIGW searches for an intermediate target geometry that can be refined into the prescribed target law without changing conditional means. Under finite second moments, minimizers exist and martingale gluing recovers an optimal coupling. With ridge regularization, moment duality gives an $A$-$B$ min-max problem whose inner step is weak optimal transport with a quadratic cost parameterized by $A$ and $B$; the outer problem optimizes these matrices. For finitely supported measures, we give an iterative algorithm. Under a quantitative ridge condition, the reduced problem is convex--concave, and the projected outer iteration satisfies an explicit contraction bound for inexact inner solves. Point cloud and graph feature refinement experiments illustrate how mean-preserving target refinements can have zero cost. A paired peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC) multiome study evaluates atlas based cell type transfer through RNA/ATAC alignment in cell to cell and prototype to cell settings, with the prototype to cell setting representing the one-to-many case.

</details>

Abstract: System operators will probably allow virtual power plants (VPPs) to submit their feasible region (FR) for market clearing and dispatch. A VPP needs to determine its FR to submit as a whole based on the individual operation model of its internal distributed energy resources (DERs), which is an FR aggregation problem. Existing FR aggregation approaches rely on analytical methods, which have issues with generality and adaptability. In this paper, we propose a data-driven approach to approximate the energy-regulation FR of VPPs. It adopts the virtual battery model to approximate the aggregate FR of a VPP and determines the model parameters through inverse optimization based on generated multi-scenario operation data using the original operation model. Numerical tests verified the accuracy of the proposed method. We believe that our work helps to better leverage the flexibility of DERs.

</details>

Method: 1) 在Hilbert复形框架下将状态分解为规范固定的局部分量与调和分量；2) 引入有限维拓扑致动器，其可达模态由域拓扑（Betti数）与致动器秩共同决定；3) 建立适定性和弱最优性条件；4) 采用FEEC进行结构保持离散化并给出保持调和结构的误差估计；5) 推广到含时Maxwell控制，将调和电环流与磁通量作为具有显式守恒和可达性性质的动态状态处理。

Conclusion: 该框架为拓扑敏感的PDE最优控制提供了统一的理论基础，表明域拓扑与致动器秩共同决定系统可达模态，FEEC离散化能保持调和结构并实现网格无关的优化，为复杂几何域上的分布式控制（如电磁场、流体环流控制）提供了严格的分析工具。

Abstract: We develop a framework for PDE-constrained optimal control on Hilbert complexes and its structure-preserving discretization by finite element exterior calculus (FEEC), with emphasis on problems where nontrivial topology creates physically meaningful global modes. We separate the gauge-fixed local state from harmonic components representing global circulation or flux, and introduce finite-dimensional topological actuators whose reachable modes are determined jointly by domain topology and actuator rank. We establish well-posedness, weak optimality conditions, reachability results, and FEEC error estimates preserving the harmonic structure. Numerical experiments on contractible and multiply connected domains demonstrate circulation and cavity- flux control, rank-dependent reachability, mesh-independent optimization, and topology-induced singular convergence. We further extend the framework to time-dependent Maxwell control, where harmonic electric circulation and magnetic flux become dynamical states exhibiting explicit conservation and reachability properties.

</details>

Abstract: Paradigmatic interaction models explain how collective behaviors can emerge in complex systems from interactions among the constituent agents. In bio-inspired swarms, however, interactions alone may not suffice to bring the population to a desired aggregate configuration within a prescribed time horizon, as needed in applications ranging from targeted therapy to collective transport and emergency evacuation. In the present work, we consider finite-horizon minimum-energy collective steering for inertial swarms that are subject to stochastic disturbances. We focus on the mean-field representations of these multi-agent systems driven by Cucker--Smale alignment or Morse attraction--repulsion interactions. Our objective is to steer the swarm between prescribed endpoint distributions using a state-feedback control, where the endpoint specifications can be full phase-space distributions (positions and velocities) or position marginals alone. Our formalism is rooted in the theory of Schrödinger bridges, which has inspired contemporary developments spanning statistical inference, biological modeling, stochastic control, and generative learning. Within the bridges framework, the uncontrolled interacting stochastic dynamics are viewed as a prior model, and the optimal control as the minimum-energy corrective drift needed to realize the prescribed distributions. We derive nonlinear, coupled necessary optimality systems with a time-symmetric structure reminiscent of classical Schrödinger bridges, and propose nested fixed-point schemes to numerically solve them. Numerical examples show that the obtained optimal control (corrective drift) can dynamically exploit or counteract the interaction forces, depending on whether the latter are favorable or adversarial to the steering task.

</details>

Result: 建立了强解的全局存在唯一性；得到F(x(t))-F* = O(t^-2)的加速目标值估计；证明原始轨迹强收敛到最小点；当解集为仿射时，极限为初始锚点的投影P_S(z0)；对秩亏最小二乘问题，动力学选择最接近z0的最小二乘解；建立了相位变量的有限加权耗散估计；在非线性反馈活跃的坐标中，若轨迹与锚点间隔分离，则相应齐次相位动力学获得额外的多项式衰减因子。

Conclusion: 该加速梯度流通过内生锚点机制实现了加速收敛、额外耗散和最小化解选择三重功能，且仅需一阶信息。特别地，它自然地解决了秩亏最小二乘问题中的解选择问题，选择最接近初始锚点的解。

Abstract: We introduce an accelerated gradient flow with an endogenous gradient-memory anchor and a nonlinear restoring--damping feedback. The anchor evolves from a weighted history of the gradients, while the nonlinear feedback is modulated by the squared coordinatewise displacement from the anchor. The resulting dynamics uses only first-order information from the objective function and does not involve explicit Hessian information. Under suitable assumptions, we establish global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions. A Lyapunov analysis yields the accelerated objective-value estimate $F(x(t))-F^\star=\mathcal{O}(t^{-2})$, with the nonlinear restoring--damping term contributing additional dissipation. Under similar assumptions, the primal trajectory converges strongly to a minimizer. When, in addition, the solution set $S$ is affine, the limit is identified explicitly as the Euclidean projection $P_S(z_0)$ of the initial anchor $z_0$ onto $S$. In particular, for rank-deficient least-squares problems, the dynamics selects the least-squares solution closest to $z_0$. We also establish a finite weighted dissipation estimate for the phase variable. Moreover, in coordinates where the nonlinear feedback is active, if the trajectory remains separated from the anchor over an interval, then the corresponding homogeneous phase dynamics acquires an additional polynomial decay factor. Numerical experiments illustrate the minimizer-selection, accelerated objective-value decay, and restoring--damping mechanisms.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线交通预测必须在连续、不完美的感知条件下进行，但缺失观测和异常扰动使得预测任务极具挑战性，尤其当预测依赖单一交通视图时更加困难。现有方法难以同时应对不完美多视图观测和在线流式部署的高效性需求。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上的实验表明，MVCTD在严重数据缺失条件下仍能实现准确预测，且运行时间具有显著优势，验证了其在线交通预测的适用性。

Abstract: Accurate online traffic prediction is essential for intelligent transportation systems, where forecasting must be performed continuously under imperfect sensing conditions. Missing observations and anomalous disturbances make this task challenging, particularly when prediction relies on a single traffic view. This paper proposes a Multi-View Coupled Tensor Decomposition (MVCTD) model for online traffic prediction from imperfect multi-view observations, such as speed, flow, and occupancy. The proposed model uses coupled tensor decomposition to build a structured latent forecasting space, in which shared spatial structures across traffic views and view-specific temporal dynamics are jointly modeled. A group sparse regularization is further introduced to capture correlated abnormal responses induced by real traffic anomalies and thus reduce their influence on forecasts. For streaming deployment, MVCTD performs iterative refinement only on the current latent tensor, while the remaining model variables are updated by lightweight closed-form steps based on summarized historical information, thereby avoiding repeated optimization over the full historical sequence. Experiments on real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that MVCTD achieves accurate forecasts with favorable runtime under severe missingness, confirming its suitability for online traffic prediction.

</details>

*Xiangkai Sun,Zeying Gao,Liang He,Kok Lay Teo*

TL;DR: 本文研究带隐式Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性原始-对偶动力学，用于求解带线性等式约束的强凸优化问题，建立了快速收敛速率，并通过隐式时间离散化推导出惯性加速原始-对偶算法，同时扩展到非光滑凸优化情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强凸优化问题在诸多领域中具有重要应用，尤其是带线性等式约束的情形。现有的原始-对偶动力学方法在加速收敛方面仍有提升空间，特别是在结合隐式Hessian驱动阻尼以获得更好的收敛性质方面。

Method: 1) 提出带隐式Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性原始-对偶动力学系统；2) 通过Lyapunov方法建立目标函数值误差、可行性度量、轨迹及速度向量的快速收敛速率；3) 通过适当调节参数实现指数收敛；4) 对动力学系统进行隐式时间离散化推导出惯性加速原始-对偶算法；5) 扩展结果到非光滑凸优化情形。

Result: 提出的动力学系统在适当参数下可实现指数收敛速率；导出的离散算法通过Lyapunov方法验证获得了与连续时间系统一致的收敛速率；数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将隐式Hessian驱动阻尼引入惯性原始-对偶动力学框架中，实现了强凸约束优化问题的快速收敛，并在算法层面保持了与连续时间系统一致的收敛性能，同时拓展至非光滑情形。

Abstract: This paper investigates inertial primal-dual dynamics with implicit Hessian-driven damping for strongly convex optimization problems with linear equality constraints. We first establish fast convergence rates for the objective function value error, the feasibility measure, and both the trajectory and its corresponding velocity vector. By appropriately adjusting parameters, we show that the proposed system achieves exponential convergence rates. Through implicit time discretization of the dynamical system, we derive an inertial accelerated primal-dual algorithm for solving the strongly convex optimization problems. Using Lyapunov-based method, we show that the proposed algorithm achieves convergence rates consistent with those of its continuous-time counterpart. We also extend the obtained results to non-smooth convex optimization case. Furthermore, we conduct numerical experiments to illustrate theoretical results.

</details>


### [84] [A Decomposed Bilevel Search for Variable-Metric Proximal Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25557)
*Xinpeng Li,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出分解双水平搜索（DBS）方法，通过对角加秩一因子分解X=D+uvᵀ构造变度量近端方法，将缩放近端步转化为二维单调残差系统，在只使用普通近端评估的情况下实现加速复合凸优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变度量近端方法可加速复合凸优化，但拟牛顿度量诱导的缩放近端映射通常没有闭式解，导致计算困难。现有方法往往需要广义雅可比或活跃集信息，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出DBS方法：(1) 采用对角加秩一因子X=D+uvᵀ，其对角缩放满足弱割线方程；(2) 诱导的逆度量B⁻¹=XXᵀ可恢复零内存DFP/BFGS类Broyden成员；(3) 因子形式将每个缩放近端步约化为二维单调残差系统，每次残差评估只需一次对角度量近端映射；(4) 认证双水平求解达到ε精度需O((d+Tp)log²(1/ε))工作量；(5) 强凸条件下外层方法线性收敛，支持精确与非精确内层求解。

Result: 在SLOPE/OWL和组套索逻辑回归实验上：内层oracle仅需几百次普通近端评估即可求解条件数高达4×10⁶的高维SLOPE缩放近端子问题；外层基准中，热启动oracle控制缩放近端开销，DBS在SLOPE基准上用远少于Lipschitz归一化FISTA的梯度评估达到严格目标精度；在real-sim上具有明显目标时间优势；组套索逻辑回归中DBS在合成相关实例上可靠且在真实分组复制实例上最快。

Conclusion: DBS方法通过巧妙的因子分解将难以处理的变度量缩放近端映射转化为仅需普通近端评估的2D单调残差系统，在理论上具有最优复杂度保证，在实践上显著优于FISTA等基准方法，特别适用于SLOPE/OWL和组套索这类高条件数问题。

Abstract: Variable-metric proximal methods accelerate composite convex optimization,
  but the scaled proximal map induced by a quasi-Newton metric rarely
  has a closed form. We develop \emph{Decomposed Bilevel Search} (DBS), based
  on a diagonal-plus-rank-one factor \(X=D+uv^\top\) whose diagonal scaling
  satisfies a weak secant equation. The induced inverse metric
  \(B^{-1}=XX^\top\) recovers zero-memory DFP/BFGS-type Broyden members, while
  the factor form reduces each scaled proximal step to a two-dimensional
  monotone residual system. Each residual evaluation requires one
  diagonal-metric proximal map, and a certified bilevel solve reaches target
  accuracy \(ε\) in
  \(\mathcal O((d+T_p)\log^2(1/ε))\) work, where \(T_p\) is the cost of
  that proximal map. Under strong convexity, the outer method converges
  linearly with exact and inexact inner solves. In the scalar
  specialization \(D=αI\), the oracle uses only ordinary proximal
  evaluations of the regularizer and no generalized Jacobian or active-set
  information.
  Experiments on ordered-weighted \(\ell_1\) (SLOPE/OWL) and group-lasso
  logistic regression evaluate both the inner oracle and the full outer
  method. The oracle solves
  high-dimensional SLOPE scaled proximal subproblems of
  condition number up to \(4\times10^6\) using a few hundred ordinary
  proximal evaluations. In SLOPE outer benchmarks, the
  warm-started oracle keeps the scaled-proximal overhead controlled and DBS
  reaches stringent targets with far fewer gradient evaluations than
  Lipschitz-normalized FISTA; on
  \texttt{real-sim} this becomes a clear target-time advantage. On
  group-lasso logistic regression, DBS is reliable on synthetic correlated
  instances and fastest on a real-data grouped-copy instance.

</details>


### [85] [Sequential Stability of the Value Function and the Solution Mapping in Berge's Maximum Theorem via Variational Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25789)
*John Cotrina,Raúl Fierro,Rubén López*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数优化问题中，当目标函数和可行映射同时受到序列扰动时的稳定性问题，通过变分近似（epi-收敛、hypo-收敛、图形收敛等）来分析值函数序列和最优解映射序列的收敛性，并应用于广义纳什均衡问题和有限时域动态规划模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berge最大定理保证了参数优化问题中值函数的连续性和解映射的上半连续性，但实际优化数据往往含有固有误差。现有研究通常只考虑单方面的扰动，本文动机在于同时考虑目标函数和可行映射的序列扰动下，系统稳定性是否仍然保持，以更贴近实际应用场景。

Method: 采用函数的下/上半连续、epi-收敛和hypo-收敛概念，以及多值映射的下/上半连续和图形收敛概念，建立这些收敛类型之间的关系，通过变分近似分析值函数序列和解映射序列的收敛性。同时提供相应的例子和反例来阐明概念边界，并将理论结果应用于广义纳什均衡问题和有限时域动态规划模型。

Result: 获得了在目标函数和可行映射同时扰动下值函数和解映射的稳定性结果，建立并澄清了多种收敛概念之间的关系（含例子和反例），扩展并补充了现有文献中的稳定性结果。在广义纳什均衡问题中通过直接方法获得稳定性，在有限时域动态规划模型中在新颖的扰动假设下证明了稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文系统地建立了参数优化问题在双重序列扰动下的稳定性理论框架，推广了Berge最大定理的适用范围，并为广义纳什均衡博弈和动态规划等实际应用提供了理论保障，填补了现有文献在处理联合扰动场景下的空白。

Abstract: Berge's maximum theorem ensures the continuity of the value function and the upper semicontinuity of the solution mapping in parametric optimization problems. This theorem plays a central role in optimization theory, game theory, and dynamic programming. Motivated by the inherent inaccuracies in optimization data, this paper investigates the stability of such problems under sequential perturbations of both the objective function and the feasible mapping. The analysis focuses on the convergence of sequences of value functions and solution mappings via variational approximations of the data. To this end, we employ lower and upper continuous, epi- and hypo-convergence notions for functions, together with lower and upper continuous and graphical convergence notions for multifunctions. In addition, we study some relationships among these types of convergence and provide examples and counterexamples associated with the corresponding notions. Our results extend and complement existing stability results in the literature. We provide applications to generalized Nash equilibrium problems, where stability is obtained via a direct approach, as well as to finite-horizon dynamic programming models under novel perturbation assumptions.

</details>


### [86] [A unified continuous-discrete framework for Nesterov acceleration: transitions between convex and strongly convex regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26014)
*Xin He,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的连续-离散框架，系统地桥接了经典Nesterov加速方法在凸和强凸两种设定下的阻尼与惯性参数选择，使得算法在早期保持凸情形下的加速收敛行为，同时最终达到强凸情下的指数/线性渐近收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Nesterov加速在凸与强凸设定下需要采用不同的阻尼和惯性参数。当强凸参数很小时，直接使用强凸设定下的系数会导致早期收敛速度劣于凸设定下的选择，尽管其渐近速率更优。需要一个统一框架来平滑过渡两种设定，兼顾早期加速与渐近指数收敛。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖经典凸与强凸情形的统一连续-离散框架。连续时间动力学由两状态耦合导出，并在统一的Lyapunov框架下分析，同时获得O(1/t^2)与指数收敛估计。通过离散化所提出的动力学，进一步导出两类加速前向-后向（forward-backward）算法，覆盖凸、强凸及中间情形。生成的系数族包含双曲、指数、代数及多项式过渡类型。

Result: 所提出的系数族在早期保持加速凸行为，同时达到强凸渐近速率。统一Lyapunov框架同时恢复了经典凸与强凸的收敛速率。数值实验表明，在强凸参数较小时所提方法有效。框架能够恢复经典Nesterov惯性系数，并生成多种过渡族。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个理论统一且实用的框架，解决了Nesterov加速在凸-强凸过渡区域的参数选择难题，为强凸参数较小时的实际应用提供了更优的算法选择。

Abstract: Classical Nesterov acceleration employs different choices of damping and inertial parameters in the convex and strongly convex settings, both for continuous-time dynamics and for discrete algorithms. When the strong convexity parameter is small, directly using the strongly convex damping or inertial coefficient may lead to slower early-stage convergence than the corresponding convex choice, despite its favorable asymptotic exponential or linear rate. We develop a unified continuous-discrete framework that encompasses both classical regimes and provides systematic transitions between them. The resulting coefficient families retain the accelerated convex behavior at early stages while attaining the strongly convex asymptotic rate. The continuous-time dynamics arise from a two-state coupling and are analyzed within a unified Lyapunov framework that yields simultaneous $\mathcal{O}(1/t^2)$ and exponential convergence estimates, thereby recovering the classical convex and strongly convex rates. We further derive two classes of accelerated forward-backward algorithms by discretizing the proposed dynamics and establish convergence estimates covering the convex, strongly convex, and intermediate regimes. The framework recovers the classical Nesterov inertial coefficients and generates hyperbolic, exponential, algebraic, and polynomial transition families. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods when the strong convexity parameter is small.

</details>


### [87] [The Value of Human Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26051)
*Bradley Sturt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的策略评估方法，利用决策者对名义问题最优值的先验信念（来自领域知识和物理世界交互）来提供更紧的性能保证，并证明当最坏情况性能计算是凸规划时，人类专业知识的价值等于一个极大极小问题的极小极大间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数未知的优化应用中，决策者通常拥有数据集无法捕捉的领域知识和物理世界交互信息，这些信息使决策者相信名义问题（即参数已知时的优化问题）的最优值不太可能很大。现有的策略评估方法无法利用这种人类知识来提供更紧的性能保证。

Method: 提出一种基于决策者信念的策略评估方法，该方法在信念正确时提供更紧的性能保证。核心理论结果：如果计算策略最坏情况性能是一个凸规划问题，那么人类专业知识的价值（即从名义问题信念中获得的最大性能保证改进）等于一个max-min问题的极小极大间隙。作者在组合优化和最短路问题中进行了实例说明。

Result: 证明了人类专业知识价值与极大极小问题的极小极大间隙之间的等价关系，并在组合优化（assortment optimization）和最短路（shortest path）两个经典问题中展示了该方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为将不可量化的领域知识纳入决策评估提供了理论框架，表明人类专业知识可以在特定条件下系统性地提升性能保证，并通过凸优化的理论工具给出了精确刻画。

Abstract: We consider optimization applications with unknown parameters where the decision maker believes that the optimal value of the nominal problem-the optimization problem they would have solved if the true parameters were known-is unlikely to be large. This belief derives from information that humans have that is not captured in datasets, obtained from domain knowledge and interacting with the physical world. We propose an approach to evaluating policies that provides tighter performance guarantees if the decision maker's belief happens to be correct. Our main result shows that if computing a policy's worst-case performance is a convex program, then the value of human expertise-the maximum improvement in performance guarantees that can be obtained from the belief about the nominal problem-is equal to the minimax gap of a max-min problem. We illustrate our developments in assortment optimization and shortest path problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [88] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，利用训练时多位置IMU（四个）的教师模型蒸馏出仅使用右臂单个IMU的学生模型。DIW通过自适应地为每个样本分配logit和特征损失的样本级门控权重，显著提升单IMU模型的动作识别性能，同时保持推理时的传感部署不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多位置惯性传感器可以提升活动识别性能，但推理时需要所有传感器会增加部署负担。作者希望利用训练时的多IMU信息来提升仅使用单个IMU（右臂）的推理模型性能，同时解决固定权重知识蒸馏中每个样本从教师目标中受益不均的问题。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的四IMU教师模型提供logit和特征蒸馏目标；2) 提出动态影响加权（DIW）：在折叠内部训练参与者上测试一步候选更新，为logit和特征损失分别分配样本级门控权重；3) 在WEAR数据集上进行主题分离的五折交叉验证。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上（19个标签，68,298个完整窗口，22名参与者），DIW达到0.638451的宏F1分数，相比监督学习（0.561820）和固定权重KD（0.571623）分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点。DIW在19个标签中的18个和22名参与者中的21名上超过监督学习。

Conclusion: DIW成功将训练时的多位置IMU信息转化为更强的单IMU模型，推理时学生模型保持相同的80,915参数架构和仅右臂单IMU传感配置，无需改变部署传感或学生前向计算图，即可获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [89] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny，一个0.6B参数的端侧法律领域嵌入模型。该模型在MLEB基准上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B以下参数模型中表现出竞争力。通过两阶段训练（教师蒸馏+领域微调）、340万查询-段落对的高质量数据（含15万人工精选样本），以及多量化等级推理架构，实现了资源受限环境下的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域检索需要专门化的嵌入模型，但现有通用模型在专业领域表现不佳，且大模型难以在资源受限环境中部署。作者旨在证明通过领域特定训练配合高质量数据，可以在紧凑模型规模下显著提升专业领域检索性能。

Method: 采用两阶段训练管道：(1) 从较大教师模型蒸馏知识到紧凑学生架构；(2) 结合难负样本挖掘进行领域特定微调。使用340万查询-段落对的数据集（含15万跨司法辖区的人工精选样本），并设计支持BF16、INT8和二值量化等多级量化的高效推理架构。

Result: GreenLeaf-Tiny在MLEB上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B以下参数规模模型中表现出竞争性性能，同时支持多种量化级别以适配资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合高质量数据的领域特定训练能够显著提升专门领域应用的性能，即使在小参数规模的嵌入模型上也能取得有竞争力的检索效果，为法律领域嵌入式部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [90] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动的视角系统综述了多模态异常检测（MMAD），将现有方法分为"正态性假设"与"异常性假设"两大互补范式，并探讨了基础模型对MMAD的变革性影响及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMAD在工业检测、网络安全等安全关键领域日益重要，但现有文献跨领域碎片化，且已有综述多按架构分类，而非依据异常的定义与分离方式来组织，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 形式化MMAD问题；识别五个内在特征作为核心挑战基础；将先前工作组织为两大范式——正态性假设方法（通过表示学习、跨模态对齐、知识增强建模规律）和异常性假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构性和语义性异常注入锐化决策边界）；并研究基础模型如何通过可扩展预训练、灵活跨模态迁移与推理能力重塑MMAD。

Result: 提出了假设驱动的MMAD分类框架，梳理了两大互补范式下的方法论体系，汇总了跨领域的代表性基准与评估协议，并识别了基础模型带来的变革性趋势。

Conclusion: MMAD领域需要更鲁棒、自适应且可解释的系统；未来应关注基础模型赋能的MMAD、开放问题以及跨领域统一的评估与基准建设。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [91] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExFold是一个训练无关的MoE加速框架，通过将prefill和decode统一为一个有预算的输出逼近问题——仅执行受约束的专家子集，同时用校准的标量投影算子将预算外专家的贡献折叠到保留专家上，实现最高1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速，同时保持约99%的平均质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽能扩展容量同时控制计算量，但低延迟serving面临两个瓶颈不同的推理阶段：prefill受token级专家计算主导，decode受batch级激活专家集的内存流量约束。现有训练无关加速方法只优化单一资源代理（token执行的专家或batch激活的专家），且要么丢弃被排除专家的贡献，要么仅隐式近似，缺乏统一框架。

Method: ExFold将prefill和decode统一建模为预算输出逼近问题：执行阶段特定的受约束专家集，同时用校准的标量投影算子将预算外专家的贡献折叠到保留专家。基于专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同的观察，在无标注数据上校准成对标量投影矩阵，推理时折叠排除专家贡献。prefill加速变为token级Top-K折叠，decode加速变为batch级专家池折叠，两者共享同一折叠机制，仅在保留专家的选择方式上不同。

Result: 在vLLM中实现为即插即用插件，配合轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，获得最高1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速，同时保持约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold通过统一的专家折叠机制同时解决MoE prefill和decode的加速问题，是首个将两个阶段的加速统一到同一框架下的训练无关方法，兼顾了加速效果和模型质量保持。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [92] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双分支频谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），将PDE求解中的低频和高频分量分离到不同的子网络中，并通过自适应门控进行路由。在五个一维基准PDE上的部分受控消融实验表明：频率分解在频谱复杂的多尺度问题上效果显著（相对L2误差最多降低59.2%），但在平滑问题上增益有限，且在某些基准上表现更差；门控的收益与目标解的频谱丰富度正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PINN存在频谱偏差问题，虽然已有傅里叶特征嵌入和正弦激活等技术，但多数研究假设这些技术普遍有效，缺乏对不同频谱区域实际受益情况的系统检验。作者希望厘清频率分解和频谱路由在哪些问题上真正有帮助。

Method: 提出DBSG-PINN架构：将低频和高频分量分别送入两个子网络，通过自适应门控（adaptive gate）进行联合与路由。在五个一维基准PDE上进行部分受控消融实验，对比频率分解和频谱路由的独立贡献，覆盖从平滑单尺度到振荡多尺度的问题类型。

Result: 频率分解在多模态波动问题上将相对L2误差降低最多59.2%，但对平滑PDE增益有限；在1D Wave基准上反而比固定组合变体表现更差。门控的收益与目标解的频谱丰富度呈正比：在多尺度基准上优势最大，在单尺度上优势最小甚至为负。

Conclusion: 频谱门控的有效性取决于问题的频谱复杂度，并非普适。该研究为探索性工作，仅基于单一训练种子和五个一维基准，需要更多种子和基准来验证这一模式的普适性，其目的在于提出问题而非给出确定答案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [93] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAMPWQ，一种基于Fisher信息的自适应混合精度权重量化方法，用于在资源受限设备上高效部署大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型资源消耗过大，难以在资源受限设备上部署。传统量化方法因均匀位宽或简单的启发式敏感度评估导致严重性能下降。

Method: 首先提出基于Fisher信息的系统模型来度量逐层量化敏感度；其次提出基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感度指标生成自适应位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准上的实验表明，FAMPWQ在PPL（最多降低3.39）、准确率（最高提升6.87%）和LLM-as-a-judge对比（最高76%胜率）上显著优于7个基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息驱动的自适应混合精度量化，在保持模型性能的同时实现高效的大模型推理，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [94] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacroAgent，一个利用大语言模型（LLMs）来发现启发式算法的四阶段宏单元合法化框架，包括聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间细化，在布局规整性、布线线长和鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏单元占据核心区域且其位置对最终QoR影响显著，但现有宏合法化方法要么缺乏鲁棒性、要么计算成本高、要么忽略了宏之间的规整性。

Method: 提出MacroAgent框架，采用四阶段流水线：聚类（clustering）、轮廓生成（contour generation）、模板匹配（template matching）和簇间细化（inter-cluster refinement），并利用LLM自动发现多个有效的、感知规整性的启发式轮廓算法。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准上，相比SOTA方法实现2-8倍布局规整性提升、3%-5%布线线长降低且拥塞相当；端到端Cadence Innovus评估显示相比DREAMPlace基线降低2.9%布线线长、改善68.3% TNS，集成到Innovus宏放置流程时布线线长降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent通过LLM驱动的规整性感知算法自动发现，实现了鲁棒、高效且面向实际PPA收益的宏合法化框架，验证了规整性改善能够转化为有形的布局布线质量提升。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [95] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAT-GS是一个面向多模态神经网络的优化控制器，通过温度缩放、EMA平滑、边际阈值门控策略、梯度预算重归一化和融合层PCGrad，在不修改模型架构的前提下缓解模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰三大失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端训练多模态神经网络时常表现出不稳定的神经动力学，具体表现为三种耦合的失效模式：模态不平衡（某一分支主导梯度优化）、不稳定门控（噪声置信度导致模态选择波动）和融合干扰（模态特定梯度在共享融合层冲突），这些都会显著降低学习效果。

Method: CAT-GS在反向传播阶段运行，不修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失。具体包括：(1) 通过温度缩放和EMA平滑对教师派生的可靠性进行校准；(2) 采用边际阈值策略在预热dropout、弱模态优先和弱偏向混合之间切换；(3) 通过封顶的梯度预算重归一化来稳定激进门控下的梯度幅度；(4) 在共享瓶颈处应用仅融合层的PCGrad以减少破坏性的跨模态干扰。

Result: 在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound、UR-FUNNY、CG-MNIST、AVE和CMU-MOSI等多个数据集上，CAT-GS在融合多模态精度上优于或匹敌强不平衡感知基线（如OGM-GE、G2D和UMT），并展现出更平滑的门控行为和更少的冲突融合梯度。

Conclusion: CAT-GS作为一种神经动力学优化的控制器，能够在不改动架构的前提下有效稳定多模态训练过程，同时提升精度和训练稳定性，具有较强的通用性和跨域适用性。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [96] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分解强化学习（RL）后训练算法中的各个步骤，在受控简化的环境中研究RL后训练背后的真实机制。作者使用策略输出分布的熵作为透镜，比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练各阶段如何塑造模型确定性，并揭示基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等因素如何影响后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL后训练已成为增强LLM推理、数学和编码能力的有力框架，但许多研究者和从业者仍将经典RL背后的原理视为"黑箱"。本文旨在解构RL后训练算法，澄清其表面之下实际发生的过程，为NLP社区提供将RL作为工具使用的入门资源。

Method: 在受控且简化的环境中隔离"可验证奖励"的RL机制，系统性地考察四个关键因素：基础模型的先验分布、奖励信号的粒度、提示分布的多样性以及模型规模。利用策略输出分布的熵作为比较工具，追踪预训练、SFT和RL后训练各阶段模型确定性的变化。

Result: 研究发现"虚假奖励"（spurious rewards）的效果取决于后训练所用的提示分布；RL后训练的成功取决于基础模型是否已在目标行为上分配足够的概率质量，这与经典RL中的探索概念相关联。

Conclusion: 本文为NLP社区提供了关于RL后训练机制的深入洞见，揭示了各组件之间如何相互作用影响后训练效果，可作为实践者理解和应用RL工具的重要参考资源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [97] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AFDBench，一个用于评估大语言模型生成气象推理的基准测试框架，包含7,732份专家撰写的区域预报讨论及三个互补指标。通过GRPO强化学习（针对温度精度、天气学正确性和格式合规性），7B参数模型在两个未见过的NWS办公室的1,033个样本上，风格对齐度从0.318提升至0.619，输入接地性从0.881提升至0.940。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成高风险气象文本时会幻觉数值，对天气预报沟通构成风险。现有基准缺乏对生成式气象推理的评估，且开源模型难以用专业NWS语域书写或忠实使用输入数据，因此需要专门的基准和训练方法来提升模型的气象专业能力。

Method: 构建AFDBench基准（7,732份专家讨论+PReal AI气象数据输入），设计三个指标：Met-Align（数值精度）、Style-Align（专业语域遵循度）和Input-Grounding（数据忠实度）。应用GRPO强化学习框架，使用针对温度精度、天气学正确性和格式合规性的领域特定奖励。

Result: 零样本评估显示开源LLM的Style-Align较低（约0.33）但Input-Grounding中等（约0.88）。应用GRPO后，Style-Align从0.318翻倍至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940，在两个未见过的NWS办公室的1,033个样本上验证了改进效果。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够有效教会7B参数模型像专业气象学家一样写作，并忠实解读AI天气数据，展示了RL在提升LLM专业领域生成能力方面的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [98] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示神经代理模型在无导数优化中表现矛盾的原因在于三个被忽视的因素（角色、半径、空间），而非训练精度。当代理模型仅作为候选解提议者（由真实目标函数最终审批）时有效，而替代求解器依赖的梯度时反而有害。通过117个基准实例和大量对照实验验证了这一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中神经代理模型在无导数优化中的效果相互矛盾：同一系列模型在某些求解器中大幅减少评估次数，在另一些求解器中却毫无作用甚至更糟。作者认为这种矛盾源于未明确三个关键因素，而非训练曲线所报告的拟合精度。

Method: 作者提出三个决定性因素：(1) 角色（Role）：代理模型提议候选解由真实目标审批则有益，替代求解器依赖的梯度则有害；(2) 半径（Radius）：模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，误差不会随邻域缩小而消失，也无法随邻域扩大而生存；(3) 空间（Room）：代理模型只能加速基础方法本身仍能取得的进展。作者形式化了半径感知的局部泛化，并将其与经典的完全线性条件联系起来。

Result: 117个基准实例中：受保护辅助将高精度求解数从67提升至84，而梯度替代使其降至65；从训练损失中移除梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148；1000次配对比较（10种噪声水平）表明不存在噪声阈值，只有提前停止的基础方法。模型信任域求解器（空间小）从88降至86，发布的插值软件保持103。蒙特卡洛库存模型中修复接受接口价值10.40成本单位，代理本身为0.00。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型在无导数优化中的收益由角色、半径和空间三个因素决定，而非拟合精度。设计正确的代理接口（候选提议而非梯度替代）能带来实质增益，但在空间已经很少的方法上附加代理可能适得其反。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [99] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过语义流（semantic flow）将每个预测时域归因到时间相关的历史滞后项，构建滞后-时域归因矩阵，并利用结构保持扰动和稀疏局部代理模型生成可读且时间连贯的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在高风险场景中广泛使用，但现有事后解释方法常忽略时间依赖性，无法提供针对不同预测时域的具体解释。

Method: (1) 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流量化模型内部表示中信息随时间演化的过程；(2) 聚合语义流构建滞后-时域归因矩阵；(3) 生成结构保持扰动并拟合稀疏局部代理模型，产生人类可读且时间连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，语义流变体在忠实度上达到竞争性或更优水平，且计算效率显著更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出需谨慎解读解释的区间。

Conclusion: 所提框架能提供时域分辨的时间序列预测解释，兼顾忠实度与计算效率，同时通过稳定性诊断为解释的可靠性提供了保障。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [100] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息误差场学习（PIEFL）框架，通过引入辅助误差网络学习当前近似与精确解之间的误差场，避免对整体解空间的持续优化，在相同计算预算下提高PINN的求解精度，且无需修改主网络结构，可兼容现有PINN模型作为通用后训练优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在求解偏微分方程时，后期优化阶段参数更新带来的精度提升越来越小，而计算成本却不断增加，存在严重的收益递减问题，需要更高效的优化策略。

Method: PIEFL在主网络达到满意精度后引入辅助误差网络，通过物理约束推导误差控制方程，使误差网络学习当前逼近与精确解之间的差异，并将学习到的误差修正与主网络预测相结合以提高精度。

Result: 在代表性PDE上的数值实验表明，PIEFL在相同计算预算下实现了更高的求解精度，验证了该方法在提升PINN性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFL通过将学习目标从解场转移到误差场，避免了持续优化整个解空间的高成本，将计算资源集中于修正已有预测误差，是一种通用且有效的PINN后训练优化框架。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [101] [GRAPE: Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation for Query-Efficient High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25116)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAPE是一个两阶段框架，用于优化高维黑盒函数。第一阶段通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验，第二阶段选择最大化条件期望下降的更新方向。理论证明梯度细化阶段单调最小化局部不确定性，且方向在尖峰化时收敛至真最速下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局部贝叶斯优化方法在高维场景中往往优先考虑下降概率而非下降幅度，导致步骤过于保守、收益甚微，浪费查询在几乎确定但改进很小方向上。

Method: 提出两阶段框架GRAPE：1) 梯度细化阶段，通过闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验；2) 进度感知利用阶段，在条件下降的前提下最大化期望减少来选择更新方向。

Result: 在黑盒对抗攻击中平均比基线快5.4倍；在大语言模型提示优化任务中，最终平均遗憾比第二好的方法低3.8个对数单位。

Conclusion: GRAPE通过进度感知的梯度方向选择，有效克服了传统局部BO方法过度保守的问题，显著提升高维黑盒优化的查询效率。

Abstract: Optimizing expensive, high-dimensional black-box functions remains a central challenge in modern machine learning and scientific discovery. While local Bayesian optimization mitigates the curse of dimensionality, existing techniques often prioritize the probability of descent over the magnitude of progress. This leads to overly conservative steps that yield negligible improvement, wasting queries on directions that are nearly certain to descend but offer little decrease. We introduce Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation (GRAPE), a two-stage framework that first sharpens the local gradient posterior via a closed-form acquisition function, then selects update directions by maximizing the expected decrease conditional on descent. Theoretical analysis proves that this gradient refinement stage monotonically minimizes local uncertainty and that the progress-aware direction converges to true steepest descent as the posterior sharpens. Empirically, GRAPE demonstrates superior query efficiency across high-dimensional tasks: in black-box adversarial attacks, it achieves an average 5.4$\times$ speedup over baselines, and on large language model prompt optimization tasks, it outperforms the second best method by a reduction of 3.8 log-units in the final average regret.

</details>


### [102] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REP-LIE，一种在微调过程中实现资源高效剪枝的新方法。该方法利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度估计权重重要性，无需完整梯度计算，并通过稳定性分数进行迭代式不重要的参数剪枝，最终在LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B等模型上取得与现有方法相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer预训练语言模型的计算和内存开销巨大，在资源受限环境中部署困难。传统剪枝方法依赖基于完整梯度的重要性估计，且需要提前微调模型才能获得满意性能，这一过程消耗大量资源，难以接受。

Method: REP-LIE利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计权重重要性，避免完整梯度计算；引入稳定性分数解决重要性估计中的随机性问题，并基于此进行迭代剪枝；剪枝后的模型通过轻量级更新进行微调，无需全参数优化。

Result: 在中规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上的大量实验表明，REP-LIE相比现有方法仍能取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE成功地将剪枝过程融入微调阶段，通过LoRA梯度和稳定性分数实现了资源高效的模型压缩，在保持模型性能的同时大幅降低了资源消耗，为资源受限环境下的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [103] [Toward Machine Learning with the Unit as a Primitive: Learning from Unit-Linked Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25118)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"单元"(unit)作为学习任务语义层面的显式原语，区别于传统机器学习中以样本为中心的隐式个体设定。学习任务需声明持久指称总体与同一性标准。监督学习的语义对象是单元条件响应律族；同质性是其特例。从数据学到的是(分词器, 共享响应律形式)对，其中分词器产生上下文单元令牌。当身份无法解析时，世界侧定律混合单元条件目标，学习者将共享形式与令牌组合。未链接的单行观测可能无法区分异质单元世界与同质合并世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习形式化以样本为中心，但多个观测或可能事件所指的持久个体（即"单元"）常常被隐式化处理。作者认为这种隐式处理导致学习任务语义不完整，特别是无法区分同质性世界与异质性（单元条件）世界，因此需要在任务语义层面显式引入单元这一原语。

Method: 监督学习形式化：1) 声明总体与同一性标准，实现值u表示选定指称；2) 语义对象为单元条件响应律族；3) 从数据学习(T_φ,R_θ)对：分词器产生上下文单元令牌，共享响应律形式读取之；4) 有可信解析器时固定单元并供给查找令牌，否则通过"单元溯因"从事实证据构造同类型令牌。

Result: 形式化结果显示：未链接的单行观测无法区分异质单元世界与同质合并世界；可信同单元对可将受限见证者分离。学习得到的是由分词器产生的上下文单元令牌与读取该令牌的共享响应律形式构成的配对结构，线性预测器是其实例。

Conclusion: 单元作为显式原语使学习任务的语义更加完整，同质性成为世界侧响应家族的属性而非学习者可仅凭样本推断的性质。学习者的规范若省略单元信息则为单元不敏感，这为理解监督学习中的身份解析与泛化问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually formalized through samples, while the persistent individual to which multiple observed or possible events refer often remains implicit. We propose the \emph{unit} as an explicit primitive at the level of task semantics. A learning task first declares a population of persistent referents and a sameness criterion; the realized value $u$ denotes the selected referent. Supervised learning is the main formal specialization. Its semantic object is a family of unit-conditioned response laws. Homogeneity is the special case in which those laws coincide; a sample-only conditional is silent as to whether the world is homogeneous or the observed law is only the marginal of a heterogeneous family. What is learned from data is a pair $(T_φ,R_θ)$: a tokenizer that produces a contextual unit token and one shared response-law form that reads it. The structured class takes that form to be a simple relation in the token; a linear predictor is the running instance. The token is the learner-side representation through which the task-side unit affects prediction, while a learner specification that omits unit information is unit-insensitive; homogeneity remains a property of the world-side response family. When identity is unresolved, the world-side law mixes unit-conditioned targets, while the learner composes its shared form with a token. A trusted resolver may fix the unit and supply a lookup token; otherwise \emph{unit abduction} forms a token of the same type from factual evidence. Unlinked single-row observations can fail to distinguish a heterogeneous unit world from a homogeneous pooled world; trusted same-unit pairs separate a restricted witness. The formal results concern this supervised specialization.

</details>


### [104] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于自回归Transformer的AI干扰抑制方法基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）tokenizer层来提升结构化干扰抑制性能，同时保持低延迟。实验以数字调制射频信号为有用信号、数字电视信号（OFDM）为结构化干扰，结果表明该方法在延迟和干扰抑制效果上均优于传统方法和前人的AI方法，并通过PESQ等音频指标验证了优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的结构化干扰抑制正变得越来越流行，因为深度学习方法能够联合考虑有用信号和混合信号（有用信号加干扰），从而优于传统方法。作者希望在前人基于自回归Transformer模型的工作基础上进一步提升干扰抑制性能，同时将延迟控制在最小范围内。

Method: 在已有的自回归Transformer模型中添加有限标量量化（FSQ）tokenizer层来改进干扰抑制性能；同时探索多种推理优化技术以加速推理过程而不过多损失精度。实验设置中有用信号为数字调制射频信号，结构化干扰为数字电视信号（一种常见的OFDM传输）。

Result: 该方法在低延迟条件下实现了比传统技术和前人AI方法更好的干扰抑制效果。通过PESQ等音频指标证明了AI方法的优势，并展示了算法在多种操作相关场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 引入FSQ tokenizer层的自回归Transformer模型能够有效提升结构化干扰抑制性能并保持低延迟，验证了AI方法相较于传统方法的优越性，并探讨了该算法在实战相关场景中的多种应用可能。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出掩蔽结构残差IVA（MSR-IVA）框架，用于处理sMRI与动态功能网络连通性（dFNC）的多模态融合问题。该框架结合共享结构表示、状态特异性残差自适应和掩蔽机制，在ADNI队列上将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，减少15.7%的不匹配依赖，并实现了受控的结构共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合中，将同一结构潜表示与多个动态状态耦合时，独立IVA会产生不相关的结构分解，而强制相同分解又会抑制状态特异性关系。此外，并非每个受试者都表达每一个动态状态，现有方法无法处理这种不完整状态表达问题。

Method: 提出MSR-IVA框架，核心包括三部分：(1) 共享结构表示捕获跨状态的共同结构信息；(2) 状态特异性残差自适应允许每个状态保留独特特征；(3) 掩蔽机制处理未表达特定动态状态的主体。

Result: 在ADNI队列上，相比独立成对IVA基线，MSR-IVA将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，并降低15.7%的不匹配依赖。对于表达两种状态的受试者，跨状态结构源平均绝对相关性为0.9177（无共享时为0.2978），证明了受控结构共享在保持源对应性的同时允许状态特异性适应。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA有效平衡了结构共享与状态特异性适应，解决了动态状态多模态融合中的关键难题，为理解脑结构与动态功能状态的关系提供了更优的分析框架。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [106] [Representing MAX functions using two-hidden-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25221)
*Zhimao Wang,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用两层隐藏层ReLU神经网络精确表示MAX_N函数的问题。通过计算机辅助搜索，作者获得了MAX_5至MAX_8的两隐藏层ReLU表示，这些表示基于$\max\{\sum\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$形式的项的线性组合。该工作独立于近期的一项类似研究，为理解连续分段线性函数的最小隐藏层数提供了新的构造性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定连续分段线性函数（如MAX_N）所需的确切隐藏层数是一个开放问题。目前下界为2，上界为对数级别，但尚不清楚正确的答案是否为常数层数（甚至可能就是2）。此前已有MAX_5的两隐藏层表示，但N≥6的情况是开放的。作者希望通过构造性方法为更多N值提供两隐藏层ReLU表示，以缩小对这个开放问题的理解差距。

Method: 作者采用计算机辅助搜索方法，寻找MAX_N的两隐藏层ReLU表示。具体来说，他们考虑一类特定形式的项：$\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$，其中内部每对坐标的最大值可在第一隐藏层计算，外部两个部分和的最大值可在第二隐藏层计算。任何这类项的有限线性组合都能被两隐藏层ReLU网络精确实现。因此，只要找到MAX_N在该形式下的恒等式，就获得了对应的两隐藏层ReLU表示。作者通过计算机搜索成功为N=5,6,7,8找到了这样的表示。

Result: 作者通过计算机辅助搜索，成功获得了MAX_5、MAX_6、MAX_7和MAX_8的两隐藏层ReLU精确表示。这些表示是独立于近期[Ruess et al., 2026]工作开发的，后者为所有N≤10提供了类似形式的表示。虽然两者在大方向上有共同的高层思路，但作者的表示与Ruess等人的工作存在一些细微差异，这些差异可能对未来研究有价值。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造性方法为MAX_5至MAX_8提供了两隐藏层ReLU的精确表示，进一步验证了"常数隐藏层（可能为2）即可表示MAX_N"这一猜想在小N情形下的可行性。作者指出其表示与独立工作[Ruess et al., 2026]存在细微差异，这些差异可能对解决该开放问题（即是否存在统一的两隐藏层表示方案适用于所有N）有启发意义。

Abstract: We study exact representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N(x)=\max{x_1,\ldots,x_N}$ using two-hidden-layer ReLU neural networks. This problem has been studied in recent years in an attempt to characterize the exact number of hidden layers required to represent continuous piecewise linear functions. The best lower bound is 2, while the current upper bound is logarithmic in $N$. It remains completely open if the right answer is a constant number of hidden layers (possibly even 2!) or not. In fact, a recent breakthrough was the representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_5$ as a two-hidden-layer ReLU function obtained in [Bakaev et al., 2026], and the case of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ was stated as open for $N\geq 6$ in that paper.
  Using a careful computer assisted search, we obtain two-hidden-layer ReLU representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$, and $\mathrm{MAX}_8$. We obtain these by considering rational linear combinations of terms of the form $\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$, where $a_r,b_r,c_r,d_r\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$. Each inner maximum of two coordinates can be computed in a first hidden layer, and the outer maximum of the two side-sums can be computed in a second hidden layer. Consequently, every finite linear combination of these terms has a two-hidden-layer ReLU realization. An identity for $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ in this form therefore gives an exact two-hidden-layer ReLU representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$.
  Very recently, two-hidden-layer representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ of the above form were obtained for all $N\leq 10$ in [Ruess et al., 2026]. Our representations are different and were developed independently. While our techniques share most of the high-level ideas presented in [Ruess et al., 2026], there are also some minor differences which may be of interest for future research on this problem.

</details>


### [107] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D³-MOPD，一种零开销的动态域调度器，通过复用训练中已有的per-domain反向KL散度信号，在线调整多教师on-policy蒸馏中的域采样比例，解决固定数据混合在不同域收敛速度差异大时浪费算力的问题。在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型上，相比vanilla MOPD，平均学生-教师性能差距缩小了97%（vs 63%），约3倍减少所需rollout步数，并在七个基准中的三个超越专家教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD）将多个领域专家教师蒸馏到一个学生模型中，但现有方法在训练前固定每域数据混合比例，忽视了不同领域收敛速度差异显著的事实——有些领域提前平台期，有些则在整个训练预算中持续改善。固定混合浪费了在快速收敛领域的算力，同时训练不足了慢收敛领域。

Method: 提出D³-MOPD（动态域调度），一个零开销的调度器。核心思路是复用训练过程中已产生的per-domain反向KL散度信号来自适应在线调整域混合比例。具体实现为：一个在训练进程外异步运行的watch进程，周期性地跟踪每个域的KL轨迹，估计剩余提升空间和当前改进速率，据此调整各域的采样比例，而不改变核心训练循环。该方法天然扩展到任意数量的域，且预期收益随域数量增加而增长（更多样的收敛模式供调度器利用）。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型从四个领域专家教师蒸馏的实验上：1) D³-MOPD关闭了平均97%的学生-教师性能差距，对比vanilla MOPD的63%；2) 达到相同峰值性能约需3倍更少的rollout步骤；3) 在七个基准的其中三个上超越了专家教师。

Conclusion: D³-MOPD通过动态域调度显著提升了多教师蒸馏的效率与性能，零额外开销且不改变核心训练循环，证明了充分利用训练中已有的KL信号进行在线调度是一种有效且可扩展的策略。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [108] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)方法，通过在训练时让潜在世界模型进行自由运行（rollout），解码每个潜在并惩罚与真实观测的重构误差，来弥合训练与部署之间的差距。该方法不增加参数，仅在训练时计算，在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提高到6.97±0.42时间单位，提升1.80倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型存在训练与部署的不一致性问题：训练时解码器在锚定到观测的潜在上工作，而部署时要在模型自身自由运行数百步的潜在上运行，两者分布不匹配导致性能下降。

Method: RDR通过在训练过程中让模型像评估时一样自由运行，对所有rollout潜在进行解码，并惩罚与真实ground truth的重构误差。这一单一损失项不增加参数，仅在训练时增加计算开销，权重为零时退化为标准目标函数，便于进行A/B对比。

Result: 在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提升至6.97±0.42时间单位（参数相同为193,568），提升1.80倍。该改进在未用于选择的种子上得到确认，并在10/10个预注册配置中以1.71-2.50倍的比率保持。

Conclusion: RDR能有效弥合潜在世界模型训练与部署的差距，显著提升长期预测能力。不过结果来自单一系统，潜在宽度增大时优势增长的扫描是描述性的，两个经典任务的控制实验属于初步结果。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决随机梯度下降（SGD）在自适应停止时间下的统计有效性认证问题。作者为强凸随机优化构造了轨迹自适应的上置信序列，同时对最后迭代点到最优点的平方距离和加权平均的次优性成立，在时间上同时有效，最坏情况下达到最优的1/t衰减率（含迭代对数因子），并能自适应真实随机梯度，使SGD在认证达到指定精度时即可停止而无需牺牲统计有效性。方法将SGD轨迹视为序贯实验，开发了新的递归置信序列技术和适用于时变条件均值、无界可预测范围自适应过程的一般时间一致性经验Bernstein不等式，并扩展到小批量SGD。实验表明，所得停止规则所需的迭代次数比自然确定性界少几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SGD分析在算法运行前预设确定性时间范围，但实际中停止决策是自适应的（基于观察轨迹）。这种失配导致根本性认证问题：固定时间的保证在数据依赖的停止时间下通常不成立，而基于最坏情况的确定性界又过于保守。因此需要构建轨迹自适应的置信序列来支持SGD的在线停止决策。

Method: 将SGD轨迹视为一个序贯实验，其观测为未知优化误差提供证据。开发新的递归置信序列技术，以及一个通用时间一致性经验Bernstein不等式，适用于条件均值时变、可预测范围可能无界增长的自适应过程。进一步将置信序列构造扩展到小批量SGD，利用每个小批量内实现的二阶矩结构。

Result: 构建的置信序列在时间上同时有效，最坏情况达到最优1/t衰减率（含迭代对数因子），并自适应实际随机梯度。数值实验表明，所提出的停止规则可将所需迭代次数减少几个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过完全可观测的轨迹自适应上置信序列，解决了SGD在自适应停止时间下的统计有效性认证问题，使SGD能够在认证达到准确度时立即停止，同时保持有效性且避免确定性界的保守性。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [110] [Learning from waste: Machine Learning for health risk prediction and computer vision-based sorting in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25759)
*Hilda Adwubi Osei,Catherine Tenewaa Osei,Desdemona Yaa Asobayire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用两种数据驱动方法（随机森林分类器和MobileNetV2图像分类模型）对加纳阿通苏地区家庭废物处理与疾病类型之间的关系进行定量验证，并实现自动化垃圾分类。随机森林模型在预测疾病类型上获得0.63的宏F1分数，而MobileNetV2图像分类模型在废物分类上达到88.2%的准确率和0.87的宏F1分数，为之前仅定性描述的关系提供了定量证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体废物不当处理是全球重大公共卫生和环境问题，加纳也不例外。此前2022年在加纳阿通苏的实地研究仅通过描述性分析发现社区感知到家庭废物处理与疾病模式之间存在关联，但缺乏定量验证。本研究旨在弥补这一空白，并探索在资源受限环境中可行的自动化废物分类方案。

Method: 采用两种数据驱动方法：(1) 使用随机森林分类器，基于废物处理实践和人口调查数据预测疾病类别，在报告患病受访者（N=69）的留出集上评估；(2) 使用MobileNetV2图像分类模型实现基于视觉识别的自动化废物分类，在测试集（N=415）上评估性能。

Result: 随机森林模型在预测疾病类别上取得0.63的宏F1分数，其中废物处理方式被确认为预测疾病类型最重要的实质性预测因子。MobileNetV2图像分类模型在废物分类上达到88.2%的准确率和0.87的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 研究为社区健康关系提供了此前仅有定性记录的定量证据，展示了自动化废物分类在低资源环境中的潜力。但同时也强调，仅靠技术性能并不能保证公共健康改善，有效的制度支持和实施同样不可或缺。

Abstract: The inappropriate disposal of solid waste remains a significant public health and environmental concern worldwide, including in Ghana. Poor sanitation and improper waste management practices contribute to substantial economic costs and avoidable deaths annually. In 2022, a field study in Atonsu, Kumasi, Ghana, reported a community-perceived relationship between household waste disposal and illness patterns, but only through descriptive analysis without quantitative validation. This study extends that investigation using two data-driven approaches. First, a Random Forest classifier was developed to predict illness categories using waste disposal practices and demographic survey data. On a held-out group of respondents who reported illness (N=69), the model obtained a macro F1 score of 0.63, with disposal method emerging as the most important substantive predictor of illness type. Second, a MobileNetV2 image classification model enabled automated waste sorting via visual recognition, achieving 88.2% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.87 on the test set (N=415). The vision-based approach offers an affordable, camera-driven alternative to complex multi-sensor systems, making it highly suitable for resource-constrained settings. Taken together, the findings provide quantitative evidence for a community health relationship previously documented only qualitatively. They demonstrate the potential for automated waste-sorting in low-resource environments. Importantly, the results illustrate that technological performance alone does not guarantee public health improvements; effective institutional support and implementation are equally necessary.

</details>


### [111] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究了标准神经架构在动态规划（DP）目标上长度泛化失败的原因。通过将有限min-plus DP等价为DAG上的最短路径（即热带多项式），并证明图、多项式和牛顿多面体三种描述在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面构成同构的半环。随后从几何角度给出两个结构性的负面结果，解释为何决策边界无法从长度T泛化到T+1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经架构在处理动态规划目标时往往无法泛化到更长的输入序列，作者希望从几何层面深入理解这一失败的根本原因，而非仅仅停留在经验层面的观察。

Method: 将有限min-plus DP建模为DAG上的最短路径问题，等价于热带多项式，其扩展牛顿多面体编码了决策边界。作者证明了图、多项式、多面体三种描述在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面构成同构半环，并通过分析半环中降维操作的性质和DAG拓扑的构造方式，给出了两个结构性的负面结论。

Result: 两个结构性负面结果：(1) 半环中两种天然的降维方式（将变量设为各自恒等元）既非单射也不总是封闭在DP内；(2) 串联和并联组合无法由更小的子DAG构造出所有DAG拓扑，即使所有仅含端点的操作也无法捕捉所有DP组合。

Conclusion: 长度泛化失败是DP结构本身的几何性质所决定的，而非单纯的学习算法或架构问题。这为理解和改进神经网络的长度泛化能力提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins.
