### [178] [Learning Continuous Regional Temperature Fields with Lead-Time and Resolution Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25823)
*Chunlei Shi,Jiong Wang,Yi-Lin Wei,Junming Hou,Jinjin Liu,Yecheng Zhang,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSTF（连续时空温度预测器），将区域近地面温度（T2M）短期预报建模为查询条件的连续时空温度场评估问题，通过神经场将预测时效和输出分辨率作为显式查询，实现统一框架下的逐时预报、中间时效诊断和可变分辨率输出。在中国东南0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上取得最优综合确定性技能，Bias降低17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的区域预报器通常在预定网格上生成固定数量的未来帧，无法满足查询依赖的预测时效和显示分辨率需求。这种固定输出约束限制了预报产品在实际业务中的灵活应用。

Method: CSTF首先将多变量ERA5历史数据编码为潜在气象状态，然后以坐标场形式解码T2M。空间位置、预测时效和输出分辨率作为显式查询输入。同时设计了空间梯度、时间差分和尺度一致性三个正则化目标，分别约束区域热力结构、逐时演化和跨分辨率一致性。

Result: 在中国东南0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上，CSTF在综合确定性技能上达到最优，Bias降低17.0%。全局范围的诊断进一步展示了灵活的时效和分辨率可控推理能力。

Conclusion: CSTF通过统一的场评估框架解决了传统固定输出限制问题，为区域T2M短期预报提供了更灵活、更精确的解决方案，在预测精度和推理灵活性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate regional near-surface temperature forecasting is fundamental to short-range weather services and downstream risk assessment. Existing deep learning-based regional forecasters commonly produce a fixed set of future frames on a prescribed grid, limiting their use when forecast products must be evaluated at query-dependent lead times or display resolutions. To overcome these fixed-output constraints, we formulate regional T2M forecasting as query-conditioned continuous spatiotemporal temperature field evaluation and propose the Continuous Spatiotemporal Temperature Forecaster (CSTF), a neural field that turns forecast lead time and output resolution into explicit queries when evaluating 2-m temperature (T2M). Specifically, CSTF first encodes multivariable ERA5 histories into latent meteorological states and then decodes T2M as a coordinate-based field. Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [179] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VINCENT框架，用于药物协同预测的可解释性分析，通过闭环扰动验证来识别跨药物的基序对解释，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物联合发现中，仅预测协同分数不够，研究者还需要知道哪些分子区域共同驱动预测。现有可解释模型虽能暴露原子或子结构级信号，但解释内置于预测器架构中，且没有在重复扰动下验证跨药物区域分数，也未将验证证据反馈以优化解释。因此需要一种化学一致、扰动稳定且与预测器行为对齐的基序对解释方法。

Method: VINCENT是一种训练后框架，包含三个步骤：1) 从注意力与梯度信号提取原子对证据；2) 将原子聚类为化学一致的基序；3) 通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对，并将验证证据反馈以精炼基序分配，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在25对文献标注子集上，VINCENT的平均基序召回率为0.826（95% CI: 0.78-0.87），基线方法为0.49-0.66。在全部71个测试对上，验证后的交互分数实现了3.36的TP/TN分离度。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证能比现有替代方法更准确地恢复文献支持的分子区域，同时产生更能反映预测器行为的跨药物交互分数。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [180] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDAR框架，一个两阶段的决策条件化时间序列模拟框架，用于大规模电商市场中的动作感知需求预测。它通过动作交织Transformer学习可控的状态转移，并结合残差校正模块利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表示，实现策略敏感且可靠的行动条件化仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商市场中，商家需要评估未来动作序列（如预算安排）下的销售结果，而非被动预测。现有TSF方法本质上是被动的，即使将操作决策作为辅助协变量，也只是优化基于历史策略的相关性外推，存在自回归惯性，混淆内生市场演化与决策导致的转移，导致策略不敏感的rollout和不可靠的反事实分析。

Method: CEDAR采用两阶段框架：Stage I中Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件化状态转移，用于计划干预下的rollout；Stage II中Residual Correction Module利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助的文本表示，将嘈杂事件描述与产品上下文对齐，校正事件驱动的偏差。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴1688的大规模真实数据集（约3200万条产品轨迹，含成对的状态-动作序列和对齐的事件信号），广泛的离线实验和生产环境在线受控实验表明，CEDAR在模拟准确性上持续优于强TSF基线，并为真实预算规划带来实际收益。

Conclusion: CEDAR通过引入动作感知的状态转移建模和事件驱动的残差校正，成功弥合了被动TSF与主动决策条件化模拟之间的差距，为电商平台的规划支持提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [181] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现联邦学习中的Edge of Stability (EoS)现象和渐进锐化是导致SCAFFOLD算法在实践中不如FedAvg的原因。在EoS状态下，SCAFFOLD对全局梯度的估计能力严重退化，这解释了其理论优越性与实际表现不佳之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构会减缓优化速度，为此设计了SCAFFOLD等不受数据异构影响的算法。尽管SCAFFOLD具有强大的理论保证，但实践中性能往往不如简单的FedAvg。作者试图解释这一理论与实践的差距。

Method: 通过广泛的经验探测来研究联邦优化中的EoS动力学和渐进锐化现象。作者比较了FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在不同架构和超参数下的EoS行为，测量了锐度（sharpness）的平衡值，并分析了SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下估计全局梯度的误差与锐度之间的相关性。

Result: 1) FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在多种架构和超参数下都表现出EoS-like动力学；2) 锐度的平衡值与学习率成反比（与GD类似），数据异构程度（而非本地步骤数）也会影响平衡值；3) 在EoS状态下，SCAFFOLD估计全局梯度的能力严重退化，锐度与SCAFFOLD的全局梯度估计误差高度相关。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习实践中表现不佳的机制在于：在EoS状态下具有高锐度时，SCAFFOLD无法可靠地估计全局梯度，这导致了其理论优势在实际中无法兑现。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [182] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过将分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在GS-LF嗅觉基准上微调，验证了"一次训练、多任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式。微调后的模型在四个不同的下游任务（跨数据集气味描述符预测、有味/无味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物区分度）中均表现出稳定的迁移能力，并在主基准上达到或超过专用嗅觉模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子基础模型已在分子性质预测中展现出强大能力，但尚不清楚在单一规范性嗅觉任务上微调后，能否学习到可迁移到多样化机器嗅觉问题的表征。本研究旨在回答这一核心问题。

Method: 在GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测基准上对化学预训练的Uni-Mol2进行微调，然后在四个互补的下游场景（跨数据集气味描述符预测、有味/无味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物区分度）上进行零训练直接评估。

Result: 微调后的模型在GS-LF主基准上达到或超过最先进的嗅觉专用基线，并在所有下游评估中持续迁移。对映体分析表明三维分子表征能够区分镜像分子，而二维图模型本质上无法做到，但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 结果支持"一次训练、跨任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式，表明化学预训练的分子表征为可迁移的嗅觉预测提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [183] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的时间序列可解释性框架 XTS（基于信息瓶颈原理），将归因解释与反事实解释整合到单一目标函数中，通过参数化变换网络生成解释嵌入实例，同时提供忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列可解释方法分为归因解释和反事实解释两大类，但两者被独立研究：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法存在严重不稳定性（产生类似对抗噪声的解释而非有意义的结构）。作者从信息论视角发现现有解释器易受平凡解和分布偏移的影响，亟需统一框架解决这些问题。

Method: 提出一个统一的目标函数，基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原理将归因推理和反事实推理桥接到单一框架中。公式化地防止平凡解释和分布外反事实。引入 XTS 框架：学习一个参数化变换网络来构建解释嵌入实例，其中保留的信息用于生成归因解释，受控的信息移除用于产生稳定的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，XTS 与最先进的基线方法对比，定量和定性结果均显示其始终优于竞争方法，产生忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释。

Conclusion: 通过信息论视角重新审视时间序列可解释性，统一归因与反事实两种解释范式，所提出的 XTS 框架在忠实性和稳定性两方面均展现出显著优势，为该领域提供了更可靠的理论基础和方法论。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [184] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务随机动力系统预测方法，通过在各任务独立的自由路由最后一层信念中组合可观测量的似然，吸收单位相关的损失尺度到似然参数中，在同一梯度步中学习。在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上恢复解析核，在异方差系统上取得最优NLL且校准相当，成本远低于调参网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统的预测通常需要多个观测值（未来状态、阈值事件、机制标签），每个都有各自的似然。标准多任务方法需要平衡各任务损失，需要调整或学习权重。作者希望避开这种人为调参平衡，同时利用随机动力学可计算的真实预测方差这一独特优势。

Method: 在共享骨干网络上，为每个任务构建自由路由的最后一层信念（belief），组合各观测量的似然。将单位相关的损失尺度吸收进似然参数中，并在同一梯度步骤中学习这些参数，避免外部调参网格。

Result: 在同方差Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上，学习的预测律恢复了解析核，正确设定的基线打平。在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63和真实空气质量数据）上，信念的输入相关方差能够分离：在状态和机制任务上取得最佳单次运行NLL，校准仅与NLL被其超越的对照相当，且成本仅为调参网格的一小部分。真实数据上状态边际在五个滚动起点上保持。

Conclusion: 该方法在随机动力系统预测中有效，利用可计算真值验证了输入相关方差的估计能力，并在效率和性能上均优于需要调参的传统多任务方法。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [185] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)的综述性论文。OPSD是一种无需更大教师模型的蒸馏方法，利用模型自身在特权信息条件下的输出来训练自己。论文指出该方法的主要失败模式是"坍缩"（生成推理路径的逐渐收窄），并提出了一个结构化框架，从三个杠杆角度（信号施加位置、特权信息性质、信号变化时机）来系统理解坍缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法虽然早期结果有前景（在较少生成token下达到可与强化学习媲美的准确率），但存在严重的坍缩问题。不同论文对这一现象使用了不同的术语描述，缺乏统一的词汇和理论框架来理解、比较和解决这一问题。

Method: 这是一篇综述性论文，不报告新实验。作者采用结构性分析方法，将坍缩视为受三个杠杆支配的症状：（i）信号施加位置——token如何被加权；（ii）教师模型看到的内容——特权信息的性质；（iii）信号变化的时机——教师动态和指导衰减。研究范围限定在数学推理领域，因该方法是该领域起源且失败模式记录最充分。

Result: 论文没有报告新实验结果，其主要成果是结构性贡献：为不同论文中命名各异的现象建立共享词汇表，并厘清了哪些结论是已确定的、哪些仍存在争议。作者明确指出坍缩并非OPSD特有，但特权信息会加剧该问题。

Conclusion: 坍缩是OPSD及类似方法的核心系统性风险，需要通过三个杠杆来理解和调控。该综述为这一领域提供了统一的语言和分类框架，有助于后续研究者更好地应对坍缩问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [186] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后验模型剪枝对稀疏自编码器（SAE）解释能力的影响，理论证明剪枝对固定SAE的影响由"扰动能量"（协方差加权范数）决定，发现幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何而严重损害SAE功能，而激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）更鲁棒；并提出基于中层敏感性的分层稀疏分配策略，在多模型架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于解释大语言模型的内部表征，但其在模型剪枝（post-hoc压缩）下的可靠性尚未被充分理解。现有剪枝方法对SAE解释能力的影响机制不明确，需要系统性研究。

Method: 1) 理论上证明剪枝对固定SAE的影响由"扰动能量"（a covariance-weighted norm）决定；2) 对比分析幅度剪枝与激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）对SAE的影响；3) 揭示所有剪枝方法共有的结构脆弱性——中间层比早期/晚期层对剪枝更敏感；4) 据此提出分层稀疏分配策略。

Result: 在四种模型架构上的实验验证了理论发现：幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何而严重损害SAE行为；Wanda和SparseGPT等激活感知方法显著更鲁棒；所有剪枝方法在中层呈现更高的敏感性；所提出的分层稀疏分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下取得更低困惑度。

Conclusion: 剪枝方法必须考虑激活几何（即扰动能量）才能保持SAE解释能力；中层对剪枝的结构性脆弱性是跨方法的普遍现象，可被利用来设计更优的分层稀疏分配策略。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [187] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力机制中的低秩适配（LoRA）秩选择提供了任务依赖的理论框架，给出了不同秩下可达近似误差的上下界，并扩展至多头LoRA和联合查询/键更新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA更新秩的选择通常是经验性的，缺乏理论指导。作者希望建立任务依赖的理论，为秩选择提供严格的数学依据。

Method: 针对预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务输入分布，推导秩r查询LoRA更新的期望KL误差下界与上界，并引入谱界（谱尾能量T_r）描述有限分数近似误差。进一步分析softmax饱和效应、多头LoRA及联合查询/键更新的影响。

Result: 证明了误差下界与ψ(‖d‖₂)成正比（ψ(t)=min{t²,t}），无条件上界为min{‖d‖₂²/4, √2‖d‖₂}；在可实现性、几何和矩条件下，最佳秩r误差介于ψ(√T_r)的倍数与min{T_r/4,√(2T_r)}之间。还发现softmax饱和可使匹配注意力函数所需秩小于匹配logits所需秩，并揭示了秩共享与查询/键分解约束的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供了LoRA秩选择的理论基础，表明秩的需求与目标更新的谱尾能量直接相关，而非简单的经验选择；softmax饱和效应和秩共享机制对秩要求有重要影响。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [188] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过特征谱探测分析揭示了正交优化器（如Muon）相对Adam加速大语言模型预训练的机制，发现损失景观的谱特征是各向异性的：头部区域不稳定需小步长，主体区域宽容可大步长。基于此发现提出Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon)及其轻量版SAMuon-lite，通过静态谱先验放大主体步长，在124M到1B参数的模型上超越了调优的AdamW和Muon基线，节省13.3%-24.0%的训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等正交优化器能显著加速大语言模型预训练，但其加速机制尚未被完全理解。作者希望通过谱分析手段理解Muon为何优于Adam和SGD，并在此基础上进一步改进优化器性能。

Method: 1) 在真实训练轨迹的检查点上，对每次动量缓冲区进行奇异值分解，在留出数据上估计每个奇异方向的损失最优步长，得到各向异性但稳定的谱剖面。2) 基于该谱发现，提出SAMuon：保持头部在Muon尺度，用静态谱先验放大主体步长。提供两个变体：完整版使用低秩随机SVD，精简版SAMuon-lite使用秩一幂迭代的两级近似。两者均不增加持久优化器状态或显著额外FLOPs。

Result: 谱剖面在批次和训练阶段间稳定，在不同优化器和模型规模上一致。SAMuon在124M到1B参数的"modded-nanogpt"模型中所有评估配置下均优于调优的AdamW和Muon基线，节省13.3%-24.0%的训练token达到相同验证损失，SAMuon-lite保留大部分收益且几乎无墙钟开销。理想化的精确白化版本保持了Muon的渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 谱分析提供了Muon优于Adam优于SGD的统一解释框架，指明了Muon均匀缩放方案的局限——未充分利用主体区域。SAMuon通过谱感知的步长分配进一步释放优化潜力，且几乎不增加计算开销，展示了谱分析指导优化器设计的可行性。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [189] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DualOPSD，一种非对称交替框架，让特权教师和学生共同适应。学生先向教师学习，然后教师沿同一学生轨迹向更新后的学生分布移动，使监督信号响应学习者且无需额外rollout。在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别比OPSD提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12点。1.7B和4B实验显示增益依赖模型规模，且所有规模下均减少截断，4B诊断显示师生间双向KL更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD（同策略自蒸馏）使用特权学生模型副本提供密集监督，但特权教师在整个训练过程中保持固定，而学生分布和输出风格会随时间变化，导致监督信号逐渐与学习者脱节，限制了训练效果。

Method: 提出DualOPSD非对称交替框架：(1)学生首先从特权教师学习；(2)教师随后沿相同学生轨迹向更新后的学生分布移动。此更新使监督信号响应学习者，且不需要额外rollout，实现双边自适应。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD比OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12点。1.7B和4B规模实验表明精度增益依赖于模型规模。所有三种规模下DualOPSD均减少截断。4B诊断显示师生间双向KL更低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过让教师和学生双方共同适应，解决了OPSD中固定教师与变化学生之间的失配问题，在数学推理基准上取得显著提升，同时减少截断并降低师生分布差异，但有效性受模型规模影响。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [190] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust CurveMoE，一种基于混合专家（MoE）的高效多范数对抗防御框架，通过低损耗路径连接针对不同扰动范数特化的专家模型，并利用贡献引导的部分更新降低曲线构建成本，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著提升了干净精度、范数特化精度及Union精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一参数配置下的多范数对抗防御方法存在两大问题：一是同时优化相互竞争的不同范数鲁棒性目标导致高昂的训练成本；二是这些目标之间往往存在不利的鲁棒性权衡，难以兼顾。

Method: 1）构建Robustness-constrained curve，连接针对不同范数特化的模型，形成低损耗路径；2）从曲线上受限位置导出干净专家和范数特化专家；3）仅对关键层进行专家化（selective expertization），其余参数在路由路径间共享；4）提出贡献引导的部分更新（contribution-guided partial updating），利用初始化梯度得分筛选影响大的曲线参数进行更新；5）从理论上界定了部分优化与完全优化之间的目标差距。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上，使用WideResNet和Vision Transformer架构，Robust CurveMoE在干净精度、范数特化精度和Union精度上均优于MSD和ERMC基线。其中，相较于最强基线，Union精度在CIFAR-100上提升2.37个百分点，在ImageNet-100上提升2.13个百分点。消融实验验证了部分更新、选择性专家化和鲁棒性约束专家选择的有效性。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE通过曲率路径连接范数特化专家并共享大部分参数，实现了在低训练成本下同时提升多范数鲁棒性，且具备理论保证，为多范数对抗防御提供了高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [191] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通道组共享（CGS）低秩近似方法，通过SVD分解与参数共享策略压缩大核CNN中占主导参数量的pointwise卷积（>87%），在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低存储开销，使大核CNN能够在内存受限的边缘设备上可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN性能优异但参数呈二次增长，阻碍边缘部署。现有工作聚焦于压缩depthwise卷积（通过低秩近似、权重共享等），却忽视了pointwise卷积才是参数主体（如RepLKNet-31B中占>87%），成为资源受限边缘设备（如4-12GB RAM手机）上的主要部署瓶颈。

Method: 提出CGS（Channel Group-Shared）低秩近似：一种基于SVD分解的参数共享策略。构造与SVD同构的结构化低秩范式，包含跨通道组共享的高参数成本下/上投影矩阵，以及各通道组特有的低参数成本可缩放对角矩阵。通过组共享设计实现显著参数缩减。

Result: 在RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK等大核CNN上的大量实验表明，CGS在竞争性性能与显著降低的存储成本之间取得有利平衡；通过缓解存储约束、降低内存带宽压力和最小化模型加载延迟，实现大核CNN在边缘设备上的可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS有效弥合了高性能视觉模型与边缘部署之间的鸿沟，指明了大核CNN压缩的关键方向——关注pointwise卷积这一被忽视的参数主导部分，而非仅聚焦depthwise卷积。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [192] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceML，一个将人类与AI智能体在同一竞赛上的开发轨迹以版本级schema配对的数据集（4,465条人类Kaggle轨迹，134场比赛，以及430条配对人类轨迹和207条智能体轨迹）。通过细粒度标注（分数、时间戳、动作、意图、编辑大小、分数效果），揭示了LLM智能体与人类专家在自主机器学习开发上的行为差距：人类专家能在数据、验证、模型、集成间灵活切换并重拾搁置方案，而智能体（Codex、MLEvolve）陷入狭窄循环。从人类实践提炼的规划提示能部分弥合行为差距并提升分数，但无法改变智能体的整体努力模式——表明指令只能填补可归因于指令的那部分差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有outcome-based基准只记录最终成绩，丢弃了开发过程，因此无法解释为何LLM在孤立编程上擅长的同时，在自主ML开发上远弱于人类竞争者。作者希望从过程层面（而非结果层面）具体化这一差距的成因。

Method: 构建TraceML数据集：在同一竞赛上配对人类与智能体工作，采用统一版本级schema记录每个代码版本。每个版本附带分数、时间戳、动作标签、意图、编辑大小和分数效果。对七场比赛用两种智能体脚手架（Codex和MLEvolve）运行，生成配对轨迹。并从人类实践提炼简短规划提示输入给智能体。

Result: 发现人类专家频繁在数据工作、验证、模型变更、集成之间切换，并会重拾搁置的方案；而Codex困于集成重加权和提交调优，MLEvolve原地突变模型，二者均缺乏人类级别的转向率和重开行为。从人类实践中提炼的规划提示能将被指名的行为拉向人类画像并提升分数，但整体努力分布仍保持智能体形状。

Conclusion: 通过过程级数据，可将LLM与人类的ML开发差距拆解为可观测、可归因的行为差异。指令干预只能弥合其中可还原为指令的部分差距，其余差距来自更深层的探索与恢复机制，需要新的方法（如更好的搜索策略、更丰富的记忆或重开机制）来真正弥合。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的自动多粒度图(Level-of-Graphs, LoGs)构建方法，用于从公共建筑平面图图像中提取结构化的关系知识表示。以147个全球学术图书馆平面图为案例，实验表明VLM生成的图与人工标注高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%），且不同粒度图在不同任务中各有优势：中粒度图在节点级区域预测上表现最佳，粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估上最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图图像蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，但自动构建任务自适应的图表示仍具挑战性，尤其是对于公共建筑而言。

Method: 定义了公共建筑布局的多粒度Level-of-Graphs (LoGs)，并提出了基于VLM的自动LoG构建方法，包括节点识别、边推断、文本解析和图粗化四个步骤。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注图高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%，三粒度图生成耗时509.3秒/平面图）。中粒度图在节点级区域预测上表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647，复杂度仅为细粒度的65%），粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中最有效（Spearman's ρ=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度的16%）。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可扩展、免标注的平面图结构化信息提取，将平面图图像转化为知识表示，增强了建筑生命周期中设计信息的利用，推进了设计信息学的发展。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [194] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMGUIA方法，将用户上下文结构化为类型化的领域特定模块（Sims），并用程序性示例约束，优于基于检索的个性化方法。作者构建了SIMBENCH基准（47个偏好条件规划任务），实验表明表示格式而非表示内容是影响AI代理个性化效果的首要设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI代理将用户视为单一实体，用扁平化配置文件拼接进提示词。当同一人在不同生活场景中持有不同优先级甚至冲突的优先级时，这种方法会失效。核心问题不在于代理缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表示的格式决定了代理能否利用这些信息。

Method: 提出SIMGUIDE方法：1）将用户上下文结构化为类型化的领域特定块（称为Sims）；2）用过去决策中的程序性示例来支撑每个约束；3）构建SIMBENCH诊断套件（47个偏好条件规划任务）；4）在参数层面，使用按Sim类型而非用户身份路由的LoRA适配器进行微调。

Result: 1）声明式Sim约束单独使用不优于RAG；2）程序性接地Sim在GPT-4o上比RAG高7.9个偏好遵从点（p=0.013），在100个τ-bench任务上跨GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.5稳定复现（p≤0.023）；3）任务匹配的LoRA微调比未适配基础模型提高12.8 ROUGE-L点；4）按Sim类型而非用户身份路由适配器额外增加7.3点，对28%路由错误鲁棒。

Conclusion: 用户上下文在代理中的表示格式（而非表示内容）是第一阶设计变量。将用户信息按领域类型结构化并附上程序性示例，能在参数级和提示级两个层面显著提升个性化性能。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [195] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）、数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤的混合架构——LLM驱动的决策引擎（LLM-DE），用于大规模供应链中的端到端决策，在实现智能决策的同时保证安全性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则和仅依靠优化的系统难以应对当前供应链的高不确定性、动态性和易受干扰特性，无法及时提供有韧性的决策。而日益丰富的数据源（如交易需求信号和非结构化干扰报告）为构建能同时推理、适应和优化的智能系统提供了机会。

Method: 提出LLM-Powered Decision Engine（LLM-DE）混合架构，核心包括四个组件：大语言模型提供语义推理能力、数学优化处理决策计算、概率预测应对需求不确定性、安全约束决策过滤确保决策符合性能和安全性保证。该框架支持端到端决策，涵盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和干扰缓解。

Result: 研究结果表明，将基于语言的推理与优化和形式化约束相结合，能够产生更智能、更安全且更可扩展的供应链决策，相较于纯数据驱动或启发式方案具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了将LLM融入运营决策系统的新架构、完整算法流程和数学形式化表述，为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了务实且理论的基�础，可在不确定性和大规模复杂性下可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [196] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2017至2025年间52项实证研究进行系统综述，评估AI聊天机器人在非洲心理健康领域的文化、语言和基础设施适应性，并提出文化适应数字心理健康（CADMH）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康问题长期被忽视，面临基础设施不足、污名化及专业人员短缺等多重挑战。AI聊天机器人作为低成本可及工具虽有全球应用前景，但其在非洲语境下的适用性缺乏系统评估。

Method: 系统综述52项实证研究（2017-2025），从文化、语言和基础设施三个维度批判性分析AI聊天机器人在非洲情境的相关性，并基于综述结果构建CADMH框架。

Result: AI聊天机器人能改善可及性、减轻焦虑和抑郁症状并扩展心理社会支持，但多数系统基于西方模式和英语设计，非洲本地化适配严重不足，存在本地相关性、包容性和可持续性方面的关键缺口。

Conclusion: 提出集成非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化和伦理保障的CADMH框架，为AI聊天机器人在非洲医疗保健中的整合提供研究、实践和政策指引。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [197] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究近端量子硬件上，在固定测量预算B下，二元量子分类器如何分配训练态数量n和每个态的测量次数S。作者证明了将有限样本与有限shot贡献分离的分布无关泛化界，并推导出最优分配规则 n* = 2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))，S* = B/n*，最坏情况达到 B^(-1/4) 的收敛率，并通过PennyLane仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近端量子硬件上估计Born概率需要重复电路执行，而量子学习实验的测量预算B是固定的。因此必须决定使用多少个不同的训练态n，以及为每个态分配多少shots S（约束B=nS），这是一个关键的实验设计权衡问题，但此前缺乏严格的理论指导。

Method: 针对具有固定或独立选择的测量算子M的二元量子分类器（理想分数为Tr(Mρ)），证明了一个分布无关泛化界，将有限样本项（∝√(d/n)）和有限shot项（∝√((log n)/S)）分离。通过最小化保守闭式替代上界，得到最优分配规则n*和S*，并在PennyLane上用2比特和4比特变分量子电路在九个合成基准上仿真验证。

Result: 推导出的替代上界与精确最小化器具有相同的渐近缩放，最坏情况泛化率为B^(-1/4)。在所有测试配置中，单侧经验泛化差距均保持在理论界之下，验证了理论的保守有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为近端量子学习系统的有限shot评估和实验前规划提供了保守的统计指导，补充了硬件级调度和电路设计方面的考虑。将保证扩展到完全自适应的shot噪声训练仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [198] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的架构，利用当前大语言模型（LLM）技术使海事信息交换模型（MIEM）和更广泛的富语义轨迹模型变得可行。该架构让操作员直接用自然语言输入观测，经LLM转换为类型化语义断言记录（SARs），累积到知识图谱中，再由第二个LLM进行推理、异常检测和假设排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义轨迹模型在国防和执法领域的采用障碍在于，操作员需要学习正式本体语言并手动将观测编码为类型化逻辑断言，这大大限制了其部署可行性。

Method: 方法包括四个环节：1) 操作员以自然语言贡献观测；2) LLM将自然语言翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SARs），即捕获n元关系的命名案例框架；3) 知识图谱累积存储SARs；4) 第二个LLM对图谱进行推理、异常检测和假设排序。

Result: 通过两个详细示例（9/11时代袭击前指示器场景和海事货物检查场景）展示了从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整流水线，证明该架构可立即可用。

Conclusion: 该架构使轨迹模型和MIEM能够以当前技术即可部署，建立了针对专有封装的现有技术先例，并将该方法建立在连接语义轨迹表示与神经流形几何的理论框架之上。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [199] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于玩家行为指纹的同一玩家验证方法，用于CS2等FPS游戏中的账号完整性审查。通过从比赛回放中提取跨准星控制、移动-停火协调、经济/购买、战斗/交战和时间节奏等维度的行为指纹，训练模型判断两个行为观察是否来自同一真实玩家。在1,330个demo、13,300个观察对和663,590个训练对的基础上，该模型达到0.931的平均ROC AUC，并通过多demo历史聚合将AUC提升至0.986。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FPS游戏中，账号完整性审查（如临时替代、代练上分、高技能玩家使用低段位账号等场景）需要判断账号近期行为是否与历史操作者一致。人工对比当前比赛与多场历史比赛效率低下，需要自动化的同一玩家验证方法。

Method: 将审查任务形式化为同一玩家验证：从比赛回放中提取单个玩家的行为轨迹作为demo-player行为指纹，覆盖五个维度（准星控制、移动-停火协调、经济/购买、战斗/交战、时间节奏），训练成对分类模型判断两个行为观察是否来自同一玩家，并评估多demo历史聚合的效果。

Result: 最终成对模型达到0.931平均ROC AUC，在不同玩家召回率上达到0.722（95%精确率）。特征分析显示最强的身份信号来自低层操作（准星控制、射击节奏、移动-停火协调）。在历史聚合评估中，K=10时AUC提升至0.986。

Conclusion: CS2 demo行为可以支持监督式同一玩家验证和账号级身份一致性建模，稳定的低层机械习惯比单场比赛表现结果更能提供身份判别信息，多demo历史聚合能显著提升验证性能。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [200] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放框架，通过本体驱动的RDF知识图谱统一工业数字线程，将11个模拟来源的异构制造系统（ERP、MES、PLM等）语义整合为图谱，并借助自动化发现引擎和LLM代理接口实现跨系统洞察。实验表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图谱连接，验证集上F1=1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行ERP、MES、PLM等异构系统，各自拥有独立数据模型和API，形成数据孤岛，阻碍整体分析、根因调查和工业4.0可追溯性。点对点集成复杂度为O(n²)且依赖脆弱，亟需统一的语义层解决方案。

Method: 采用五阶段ETL管线和本体驱动的RDF知识图谱实现数据统一，涵盖78个RDFS类、108个对象属性和243个数据属性，融合ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、AAS、RAMI 4.0等标准。自动化发现引擎整合九种策略类别（跨站关联、告警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等），并通过287个MCP工具将图谱暴露给LLM代理。

Result: 阻断24个跨系统工具后召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），证明69%的可发现信号依赖跨系统图谱连接；留一法消融确认六种策略贡献独特信号；65信号清单验证集F1=1.00（95%CI [0.79, 1.00]）；五个行业模板展示模式稳定性。

Conclusion: 语义图统一框架能有效打破制造数据孤岛，跨系统图谱连接对信号发现至关重要，且该方案在不同制造垂直领域具备schema稳定性。但验证清单为作者自建，属于验证而非独立验证，需进一步独立评估。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [201] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源图检索增强生成引擎，通过统一 PostgreSQL 存储、提取时不变式校验和时间演化层，分别解决了传统 GraphRAG 在基础设施、图质量和时间累积三方面的开销问题，在同等条件下相比 LightRAG 构建了更稠密、更可查询且支持时间演化的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图的检索增强生成虽然在连接跨段落事实方面有优势，但存在三大代价：一是需要维护向量库、图数据库和文档库三套基础设施并保持一致；二是提取管线从不拒绝输出，导致图中充满无实际断言意义的边，图质量低下；三是图只累积不演化，无法区分过时事实与当前事实。

Method: 将文本块嵌入、规范实体图和社区摘要统一存放在单个 PostgreSQL 数据库中，用 pgvector 做搜索、边表做遍历。在写入前执行提取时不变式校验：拒绝模糊谓词、代词性名称和裸数量；将谓词归一化到可选词汇表；实体通过模型提供的别名解析为每个规范名称对应一个顶点；否定关系通过在正向谓词上加否定标志处理。加入时间层，使关系携带来自文本的有效期，允许后续文档仅凭文档顺序使更早的不兼容断言失效，并支持 as-of 时间点查询。

Result: 在三个语料上、使用相同的提取和嵌入模型对比 LightRAG：post-graph-rag 构建的图更稠密，关系数每实体最高达 2.4 倍；图更可查询，不同边标签占关系比例在 0.46 至 0.58 之间（受控词汇下为 0.11），而 LightRAG 为 0.77 至 1.33；查询延迟更低且回答质量相当；支持时间演化，在新型序列和十年申报文件中分别有 13 和 8 条关系被取代，而基线为零。

Conclusion: 作者明确将这些定位为工程测量而非基准结果，表明该方法在工程层面解决了传统 GraphRAG 的三大痛点，尤其是在图密度、可查询性和时间演化能力上的实质改进。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [202] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FrontierChallenge，一个跨领域的端到端科学工作流基准测试，包含300个任务（已发布97个），覆盖化学、材料、生命科学等领域。评估12个前沿模型和3种agent脚手架后发现：最佳配置的通过率仅为20.6%，且部分进展分数高（如87.6）与完全交付率低（如4%）之间存在巨大差距，说明高部分得分和自信的完成声明都不能可靠地表明科学任务已完全交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学agent基准大多只强调最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，缺乏对端到端科学工作流和多领域交付完整性的评估，无法真实反映agent在科研实践中的表现。

Method: 构建FrontierChallenge基准，包含300个端到端科学工作流（发布97个），覆盖6个科学领域；每个任务提供固定输入和必须交付的完整交付物集合；使用Pass Rate（完全完成标准）和Avg. Score（部分进展）两个指标，对12个前沿模型结合3种agent脚手架进行评估。

Result: 最佳配置仅完成97个已发布任务中的20个（Pass Rate 20.6%）；分析化学和电化学/环境领域的部分进展分数很高（87.6和94.9）但完全通过率极低（4%和0%）；75.5%的未通过Claude Code轨迹以声称完成而告终。

Conclusion: 高部分分数和自信的完成声明都不能可靠指示科学任务的完全交付，科学agent评估必须结合端到端工作流执行与交付物完整性来综合判断，而非仅依赖单一指标或模型自述。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [203] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有omega-正则目标（涵盖可达性、安全性和LTL目标）的鲁棒POMDP（RPOMDP）的求解问题。研究表明，具有多面体不确定集的(s,a)-矩形RPOMDP与部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）在语义上等价，并首次建立了两个方向的归约。基于此，推导出了一系列关于求解不同omega-正则目标下RPOMDP的新计算复杂度上下界，并进一步推导了RMDP的新计算复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典POMDP假设转移概率精确已知，但在实际环境中这些概率往往存在不确定性。RPOMDP通过不确定性集来建模这种不确定性，但现有的RPOMDP求解工作主要局限于特定目标（如可达性），缺少对更一般的omega-正则目标的研究。

Method: 本文通过将(s,a)-矩形RPOMDP（具有多面体不确定性集）归约为POSG，以及将POSG逆向归约为RPOMDP，建立了两个问题类别之间的双向归约关系，从而实现了语义等价的证明。利用这一等价性，借鉴POSG已有的复杂度分析结果来推导RPOMDP的复杂度上下界。

Result: 首次建立了RPOMDP与POSG之间的双向归约，证明了二者的语义等价性。基于此等价性，推导出了求解具有不同omega-正则目标的RPOMDP的计算复杂度上下界，并作为推论获得了RMDP的新计算复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了RPOMDP在一般omega-正则目标下求解的理论空白，建立的RPOMDP与POSG之间的等价性为未来利用POSG的已有算法和理论工具来解决RPOMDP问题提供了新的途径，同时也深化了对RMDP复杂度的理解。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [204] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CVE-SAI框架，将多模态产品模型的属性推断与索引收录分离，通过反事实视觉证据引导的选择性索引机制，在保证属性推断准确性的同时，严格控制不准确或视觉不支持的值进入持久索引，从而提升检索性能并限制索引污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态产品模型在补全电商属性时存在三大缺陷：1) 仅优化属性-答案准确率而不验证视觉支撑；2) 混淆瞬时预测与持久索引收录；3) 缺乏对事实错误或视觉不支持值的显式风险控制。这些问题导致检索性能受损和索引污染累积。

Method: CVE-SAI框架包含以下核心组件：1) 先从主图像和属性问题推断并冻结本体约束的候选值（不依赖目录文本）；2) Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) 通过受控反事实干预构建属性特定视觉依赖代理；3) Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) 利用代理精化本体约束评分；4) 在证据必要性、证据保留、干扰变换稳定性和候选特定目录文本冲突审计后冻结候选；5) 目录文本只能收紧准入不能修改候选；6) 独立族级校准在5%不安全收录预算下选择单一策略并给出同步单侧有限样本界。

Result: 在Amazon Berkeley Objects的五个视觉属性上，CVE-SAI改进了属性推断和证据定位，在共享风险协议下取得最高认证收录覆盖率，并在自动收录系统中获得最强受控检索性能与最低不安全自动诱导暴露。实验证明将推断与收录分离能改善视觉支持的属性补全并限制持久索引污染。

Conclusion: 将瞬时预测（推断）与持久操作（索引收录）解耦，配合反事实视觉证据审计和风险校准，是提升多模态属性补全安全性与检索效用的有效范式。这种分离设计能实现视觉支持的属性完成与检索改善的平衡，同时限制索引污染。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [205] [Using profiles of cognitive capability to assess AI suitability for workplace tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25623)
*Jonathan Prunty,Marko Tešić,Patrick Quinn,José Hernández-Orallo,Lucy Cheke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于核心认知能力的管道框架，通过将AI代理的能力画像与任务需求权重相结合，解决组织部署AI时的任务范围界定问题（哪些任务可自动化、哪些保留给人类、哪些人机共享）。该方法在合成代理上验证了能力恢复，评测了六个AI系统，并从六个职业领域的410名员工中获取了任务需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在部署AI时面临范围界定问题：聚合基准分数无法反映系统在实际中的成败表现，而人类对模型能力的判断很快过时。需要一种可更新的、细粒度的评估框架来匹配AI能力与具体工作任务需求。

Method: 提出两条核心组件：(1) 认知能力画像——从标注了各项认知需求的基准测试中推断AI代理的能力；(2) 任务需求加权——由领域专家评估这些认知能力对其工作的相对重要性。两者共用一套核心认知维度，可独立更新并组合，生成领域、组织、角色或个人职责级别的AI适用性评分。

Result: 在合成代理上验证了能力恢复；评测了六个AI系统；从六个职业领域的410名员工中获取任务需求。发现AI系统在不同认知维度上的差异大于不同模型家族之间的差异，而工作场所活动收敛于共用的认知核心。生成的评分可作为比较性的范围界定工具。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了有效的AI适用性比较工具，可用于识别试点候选和当前系统不太适合的领域。未来工作可扩展该框架以同时画像人类工作者与AI系统，从AI适用性走向人机任务分配。

Abstract: Organisations deploying AI face a scoping problem: which tasks can be automated, which should remain with humans, and which are best shared between the two. Aggregate benchmark scores provide little insight into where systems will succeed or fail in practice, while human judgements of model capabilities quickly become outdated. We introduce a pipeline that profiles agents and tasks using a shared set of core cognitive capabilities. Cognitive capability profiling infers an agent's capabilities from performance on a benchmark battery annotated for the cognitive demands of each item. Task requirements weighting elicits from domain experts the relative importance of these same capabilities for their work. As both use a common set of cognitive dimensions, they can be updated independently as models and roles change, and combined to estimate AI suitability at the level of a domain, organisation, role, or individual duty. We validate capability recovery on synthetic agents, profile six AI systems, and elicit task requirements from 410 employees across six occupational domains. AI systems differed more across cognitive dimensions than across model families, while workplace activities converged on a shared cognitive core. The resulting scores provide a comparative scoping tool for identifying promising candidates for piloting and areas where current systems are unlikely to be well suited. We discuss extending the framework to profile human workers alongside AI systems, moving from AI suitability towards human-machine task allocation.

</details>


### [206] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套生产级的"检索-匹配"级联系统用于产品链接（实体消解），根据难度分配计算资源：检索层召回候选，轻量级文本交叉编码器自动解决高置信度多数案例，多模态视觉语言模型代理处理模糊疑难案例（通过查看产品图片和网络搜索获取证据）。交叉编码器从数百万双VLM共识标签中蒸馏而来，无需人工标注，校准后达到98%精确率自动接受阈值。自托管开放权重代理模型在接近前沿闭源VLM精确率（88% vs 92%）的情况下，每对成本仅为其1/7，无需微调。端到端链接覆盖率从低成本阶段的68%提升到77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电商市场规模的背景下，数十亿条嘈杂的多类别商品记录需与数千万规范产品消解，单一模型评分所有候选要么对难案例太弱，要么对简单案例成本过高，需要一种按难度分配计算资源的方案。

Method: 采用三级检索-匹配级联流水线：1) 检索层快速召回可能的匹配；2) 轻量级文本交叉编码器自动处理高置信度多数案例（从数百万双VLM共识标签蒸馏，无需人工标注，并校准到98%精确率）；3) 自托管开放权重多模态VLM代理处理模糊剩余案例，通过检查产品图片和发起网络搜索收集记录中不存在的证据。

Result: 代理模型在无微调情况下达到前沿闭源VLM的精确率，但召回率低4个百分点（88% vs 92%），每对成本约为其1/7。每对成本跨度近五个数量级，从廉价交叉编码器到前沿VLM，仅将难度尾部升级给代理即可将端到端链接覆盖率从68%提升至77%。

Conclusion: 通过级联架构将计算成本与案例难度匹配，可以在保持高质量的同时大幅提升端到端产品链接覆盖率，证明了"难例才用重模型"策略在生产规模下的有效性。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [207] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LifePlanner基准，将地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子相结合，通过MCP工具集提供访问，用于测试LLM智能体在地理空间规划（如行程设计）中的表现。实验发现前沿LLM在简单检索上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降（通过率降至40.2%），主要失败源于证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确和约束整合薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间规划基准只提供干净的地理数据和工具，缺少人们在日常规划中使用的非结构化社交信号，无法真实反映LLM智能体在实际场景中面对嘈杂证据和多约束推理的挑战。

Method: 引入LifePlanner基准，将地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子结合，通过MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具集提供访问，并构建涵盖四个任务类别和三个难度级别的评估套件。

Result: 实验表明，前沿LLM在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能显著下降，Pass Rate降至40.2%。失败原因主要是大规模多模态数据库中的证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确以及约束整合薄弱，而非模型规模或推理长度限制。

Conclusion: 未来改进方向应聚焦于有效的接地规划（grounded planning）能力，而非单纯依靠模型规模扩展，提示了地理空间规划领域LLM智能体发展的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [208] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SimVerity框架，系统量化了模拟评估到物理部署的"结果迁移"可信度。通过匹配场景回放、物理目击者交叉验证，发现模拟通过不能保证物理部署成功，并展现了风险预测、代理可审计性及独立模拟器局限性等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然模拟评估广泛用于AI代理基准测试，但模拟通过能在多大程度上证明物理部署成功，这一核心问题从未被系统量化。现有评估将部署成功视为模拟中的静态属性，忽视了真实部署的动态过程。

Method: 构建SimVerity"判定迁移保证"框架：(1)在目标智能家居部署中回放匹配场景；(2)以独立合格物理目击者交叉验证代理执行；(3)区分完成度、报告状态、可观测效果和最终结果四个维度；(4)从实测试验学习风险画像并锁定用于预测；(5)引入第二合格模拟器检验独立性。

Result: (1)先进模拟器通过全部240个灯光试验，但摄像头捕获42次亚秒级失败；(2)风险画像能预测未物理测量的失败路径，在11个保留会话中击败盲基线；(3)切换代理循环的模型客户端/服务配置将场景匹配率从52-88%提升至100%；(4)第二模拟器从不与主模拟器分歧，只有物理测量揭示共同盲点。

Conclusion: 部署成功是真实世界过程而非模拟静态属性；模拟器之间的共识不等于正确性；SimVerity将判定迁移转化为明确的"通过/弃权/升级"决策，为AI代理部署前评估提供了系统化保障框架。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [209] [FLARE: Verifying MILP Reformulations with LLM-Based Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25220)
*Henry Robbins,Connor Lawless,Madeleine Udell,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE方法，利用LLM智能体和Lean证明助手对MILP重构公式进行机器可验证的可靠性检查，并引入FormulationBench基准数据集，在NP-hard子集上达到100%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MILP是组合优化的基本工具，但设计高效公式是核心挑战。LLM虽能自动化建模过程，但现有方法只能数值评估公式，无法对一般问题实例进行推理验证，缺乏可靠的自动化验证手段。

Method: 引入可在Lean中形式化并机器检查的MILP重构构造性定义；开发FLARE，使用基于LLM的智能体与Lean证明助手验证提出的重构相对于参考公式的正确性；同时提出FLARE-NL作为无需正式证书的快速廉价LLM代理。

Result: 在包含20个问题和109个公式的FormulationBench数据集上，FLARE在NP-hard子集上达到100%准确率，优于现有方法，并为接受的每个重构生成可机器检查的证书。

Conclusion: FLARE通过形式化验证解决了MILP重构的可靠性问题，FLARE-NL提供了无需证书的高效替代方案，两者共同实现了自动化优化建模中的可靠验证。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental tool for combinatorial optimization with extensive real-world applications. A central challenge is designing computationally efficient MILP formulations. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to automate the modeling process, from deriving formulations to strengthening them. Reliable automation requires robust methods for verifying that proposed formulations preserve the underlying optimization problem. However, existing approaches evaluate formulations numerically and fail to reason about general problem instances. We resolve this limitation by introducing a constructive definition of MILP reformulation that can be formalized in Lean and machine-checked. We develop FLARE (Formulation-Level Automated Reformulation Evaluation), a method that uses an LLM-based agent and the Lean proof assistant to verify proposed reformulations against a reference formulation. To evaluate our approach, we introduce FormulationBench, a challenging dataset of 20 problems and 109 formulations. FLARE outperforms existing methods, with 100% accuracy on the NP-hard subset of FormulationBench. Furthermore, FLARE produces a machine-checkable certificate for every reformulation it accepts. For cases where formal guarantees are not necessary, we introduce FLARE-NL, a fast and cheap LLM proxy that matches FLARE's accuracy but produces no certificate. These methods enable reliable verification in automated optimization modeling.

</details>


### [210] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"借用权威"(Borrowed Authority)攻击类别：恶意或误用的Skill可通过附加权限声明驱动物理执行，而现有Skill格式缺乏类型化的拒绝机制。作者提出Edge Skillguard——内嵌于Skill工件中的类型化权威层，在真实边缘控制平面测试中100%拦截5种攻击变体（60/60）且不阻塞良性请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化Skill体系（AutoSkills、Hermes Agent）追求的是效率而非安全。Skill描述"行为方式"，Policy决定"行为是否被允许执行"，但当前格式只覆盖前者，后者完全交给模型。生成更多Skill只会扩大安全差距而非缩小——错误调用可能导致开门或转钱等物理后果。已存在两类相邻攻击（恶意Skill攻陷云软件、越狱LLM控制机器人造成物理伤害），但其交叉领域（恶意代理Skill造成物理伤害）尚未被报告。

Method: 提出Edge Skillguard：一个类型化权威层，区别于工作流引擎的"工具间"位置，它内嵌于Skill工件内部，在world state和sensor evidence上设置guard。在真实边缘控制平面测试床上，针对5种攻击变体进行验证，并扩展至5倍规模及跨主机Tailscale mesh场景。

Result: Guard成功拒绝60/60个借用权威请求（覆盖5种攻击变体），且不阻塞良性请求；结果在5倍规模和跨主机Tailscale mesh上依然成立。

Conclusion: 高风险Skill应将类型化调用策略与程序性知识共同打包，使物理动作依赖机器可验证的证据而非对等代理的声明。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model.
