The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [244] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动定理证明器的证明构建过程建模为形式演算诱导的转移系统上的策略，用图神经网络对证明编辑进行评分，通过模仿学习训练，在固定步数预算下比leanCoP多解决46%的问题且步数少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明器逐步构建证明，每一步需要决定添加或删除什么，但传统系统缺乏统一的策略学习框架，难以直接应用策略学习方法。

Method: 将证明构建建模为形式演算诱导的转移系统上的状态化策略，统一leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划；用图神经网络对证明编辑评分，通过模仿学习从已发现的证明中训练。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1上，学习的策略比leanCoP多解决46%的问题，且达到证明所需的步数少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 将定理证明视为策略学习问题并用GNN+模仿学习训练是有效的，可以显著提升证明效率和覆盖率。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [245] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace Integrity（轨迹完整性）作为LLM数据代理的部署可靠性评估标准，并引入CAIT率来衡量仅看答案准确率时被掩盖的计算无效输出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据任务中，LLM数据代理可能产生基准测试中"正确"的答案，但其背后的计算轨迹却是无效的。仅依赖答案准确率作为可靠性信号存在严重缺陷，无法反映真实部署中系统可审计性和可执行性的需求。

Method: 提出Trace Integrity标准（包含显式性、可执行性、模式有效性、算子忠实性、可重放性、答案一致性和可审计性七个维度），通过执行契约（execution contracts）将用户意图绑定到模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案关联，并引入CAIT率量化仅答案评估造成的虚假成功。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev上的实验显示，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，但Trace Integrity通过率分别为39%、43%和40%，CAIT率分别高达55%、59.1%和45.8%，证明答案准确率、轨迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个不同的评估信号。

Conclusion: LLM数据代理不应仅根据输出是否匹配参考答案来评估，而应评估其输出是否由可审计的计算过程支撑。答案准确率不足以保证部署可靠性，Trace Integrity应成为核心评估标准。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [246] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SwarmWorld框架，让初始同质的LLM智能体在无需预设角色或指令的情况下，通过共享空间环境自发自组织，演化出技术性社会，并能够构建出比独立搜索基线更广泛、更有韧性的技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管群体智能可以通过协调个体与共享环境交互而产生，但现有的大多数多智能体系统依赖直接对话、预设角色或集中式工作流。目前尚不清楚去中心化的智能体能否自主构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索的表现。

Method: SwarmWorld将认知与后果分离：智能体在固定动作和材料模式下提出架构和控制器，由确定性模拟器在智能体被移除后、在未见过干扰下评估功能。初始同质的LLM智能体自主探索空间环境、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久化工件并编写可执行的控制器。支持物理stigmergy及显式文化机制。

Result: 共享社会比强best-of-N独立搜索基线发展出更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合，但独立搜索在最强工件的单项性能上仍有竞争力。智能体分化为探索、构建、维护和协调等行为，并随世界成熟而转换。技术通过协作构建、可执行继承和持久的智能体-工件网络累积，多数复用始于物理观察而非交流。

Conclusion: 物理stigmergy单独即可支撑能力健全的社会；显式文化机制能增强协作与组织，但功能收益取决于结果与时间尺度。交互驱动的是持久的技术生态，而非普遍更优的个体发明。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [247] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行星预测引擎（PPE），一个能够直接从自然语言查询出发，自主执行端到端地理空间建模工作流的AI系统。PPE实时检索多模态数据集（涵盖开放网络与地球观测平台），融合地理空间基础模型嵌入，并自动搜索任务适配的模型架构，在多个跨任务、跨地域、跨学科的应用中显著优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从粮食安全、灾害风险到疾病暴发和社会经济脆弱性等全球性挑战需要高保真地理空间建模，但当前构建预测性行星模型受限于碎片化的数据生态系统，需要大量人工数据检索、多模态数据整理融合以及反复的模型选择，技术门槛高且效率低下。

Method: PPE是一个自主AI系统，核心流程包括：(1) 从自然语言查询出发自动执行端到端工作流；(2) 实时综合多模态数据集，从Data Commons和Google Earth Engine等开放网络与地球观测平台检索时空相关协变量；(3) 融合地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）；(4) 在任务定制化模型架构家族中进行自动搜索并配备过拟合防护机制。

Result: PPE在多个任务中持续优于现有最优或人工调优的专家基线：美国空间回归中，21项CDC健康指标平均R²从60.0%提升至76.8%，FEMA国家风险指数从60.0%提升至64.9%，社会脆弱性指数从58.6%提升至66.2%；尼日利亚粮食安全指标的空间降尺度中R²从31.5%翻倍至66.1%；2026年刚果民主共和国Bundibugyo埃博拉疫情流行病学即时预测中Recall@10达83.3%（五次周预测中识别出18个新入侵卫生区中的15个），较公开最优模型的约73%提升10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将自主多模态行星数据发现与针对性模型优化相结合，PPE显著降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，实现了快速、定制化、专家级水平的部署，为地理空间预测建模提供了可扩展的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Endogenous Selection and Spillovers: Bayesian Inference for Policy-Relevant Causal Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25720)
*Duong Trinh*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的计量经济学框架（Spillover Roy模型），用于在存在内生治疗选择和网络溢出效应的情境下识别和估计政策相关因果效应，并应用于美国机会区项目，发现其直接影响显著但溢出效应有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断方法依赖无混杂性或无干扰假设，在大型网络或空间情境中，当治疗选择和溢出效应同时存在时均不适用，需要新的框架来联合建模内生选择和网络溢出。

Method: 引入Spillover Roy模型，联合建模内生治疗选择与潜在结果，通过低维暴露映射捕捉邻居治疗的溢出效应；使用带参数扩展的贝叶斯数据增广算法进行高效后验计算和不确定性量化。

Result: 对美国机会区项目的应用发现：住房开发存在正向直接影响但溢出收益有限；反事实政策分析显示项目扩展的边际收益递减。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效分解政策相关的直接、溢出和总效应，为存在网络溢出和内生选择的政策评估提供了新的方法论工具，但对机会区项目的实证结果表明政策扩张的收益有限。

Abstract: This paper develops a new econometric framework to identify and estimate policy-relevant causal effects in contexts with endogenous selection into treatment and spillovers within single large networks or spatial settings. Conventional causal inference methods relying on either unconfoundedness or no-interference assumptions are generally inadequate in these scenarios. We introduce a Spillover Roy model that jointly models endogenous treatment selection and potential outcomes while allowing spillovers through a low-dimensional exposure mapping of neighbors' treatments. The model captures heterogeneous treatment responses across levels of latent resistance to treatment and neighborhood exposure. Within this framework, we define policy-relevant direct, spillover, and total effects under feasible policy changes and show that the total effect decomposes into a direct component from policy-induced participation and a spillover component from policy-induced changes in neighborhood treatment exposure. For estimation and inference, we develop a Bayesian data-augmentation algorithm with parameter expansion that enables efficient posterior computation and coherent uncertainty quantification for heterogeneous causal effects and policy counterfactuals. An application to the U.S. Opportunity Zones program finds positive direct effects on housing development but limited spillover benefits, while counterfactual policy analysis reveals diminishing returns from program expansion.

</details>


### [249] [Nonparametric Bayesian Inference for Partially Identified Discrete Response Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25814)
*Elie Tamer,Christopher D. Walker*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于部分可识别离散响应模型的非参数贝叶斯推断框架。核心思想是利用这些模型将简化形式的条件选择概率映射到识别集合，从而对条件概率质量函数进行非参数贝叶斯推断，进而实现对识别集合的贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分可识别模型（如条件矩不等式）在贝叶斯推断方面存在挑战，通常需要将条件矩转化为无条件矩或对协变量离散化，这会导致信息损失或计算复杂。作者希望构建一个无需这些近似处理的非参数贝叶斯推断框架。

Method: 该方法利用离散响应模型将条件选择概率映射到识别集合的性质，对条件概率质量函数施加非参数贝叶斯先验（基于高斯过程），从而实现识别集合的贝叶斯推断。该框架涵盖条件矩不等式和线性系统（含未知系数）作为特例。后验采样可通过闭式形式完成。

Result: 作者证明了：(1) 模型正确设定时，后验对真实识别集合具有一致性；(2) 后验能一致地检测模型误设定；(3) 在误设定下后验对伪识别集合一致。此外验证了基于高斯过程先验的假设条件，并证明该框架可扩展到连续响应和聚合离散响应（如市场份额）。

Conclusion: 该框架为非参数贝叶斯推断部分可识别模型提供了理论保障，在计算上具有闭式后验采样的优势，且灵活性与频率学派的局部识别方法相当，为部分可识别离散响应模型提供了新的推断工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a nonparametric Bayesian inference framework for partially identified discrete response models. The key observation is that these models map a reduced-form conditional choice probability to an identified set. Consequently, nonparametric Bayesian inference for the conditional probability mass function leads to Bayesian inference for the identified set. The inference framework nests conditional moment inequalities and linear systems with unknown coefficients as special cases. Importantly, our proposal does not require converting conditional moments into unconditional moments or discretizing covariates. We show that the posterior is consistent for the true identified set when the model is correctly specified, show that the posterior can consistently detect model misspecification, and show posterior consistency for a pseudo-identified set that is valid under misspecification. We also verify the assumptions for a class of priors based on Gaussian processes that we use to implement our proposal. These priors offer similar flexibility to frequentist partial identification methods, and are computationally attractive because posterior sampling can be performed in closed-form. We also show that many of the ideas in this paper extend to continuous responses and aggregated discrete responses (e.g., market shares).

</details>


### [250] [Cross-Section Estimation of Long-Run Relations Using Time-Compressed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25901)
*Serena Ng,Nikolay Gospodinov*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文分析了一类时间压缩的I(1)数据（在平均值或长期差分数据上的横截面回归），证明其具有放大变异性，可产生超一致且渐近正态的估计，收敛速率最快可达√N·T。负责任的压缩方案需同时放大非平稳信号并稀释回归噪声。但均值回归或收敛的强作用力会削弱扇出效应（如温度数据）。三个应用显示消费-收入、增长/通胀-人口统计变量的长期关系较稳健，而增长-升温关系则较脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量实证研究基于平均值或长期差分数据（即时间维度被压缩的T×N面板）进行横截面回归来探究长期关系，但这些方法的统计性质尚未被系统分析，需要理解时间压缩I(1)数据的内在特性及其对估计量的影响。

Method: 理论上分析时间压缩I(1)数据，揭示其横截面方差随时间"扇出"的放大效应；推导误差项、估计量的收敛速率（最快√N·T）；比较不同压缩方案对信号放大和噪声稀释的作用；分析省略固定效应的影响，以及均值回归/收敛力对扇出效应的削弱。

Result: 时间压缩数据中的横截面回归估计可具备超一致性和渐近正态性，无论回归元是平稳、非平稳还是高度持久的；最快收敛速率√N·T需要能同时放大非平稳信号并稀释噪声的压缩方案；省略固定效应会阻碍噪声稀释但估计仍超一致；强均值回归力会削弱扇出效应。三个应用中消费-收入和增长/通胀-人口变量关系较稳健，增长-温度关系脆弱。

Conclusion: 时间压缩的横截面回归可以是一种有效的长期关系估计工具，但其有效性取决于压缩方案的设计和数据本身的非平稳特性；温度数据中强收敛趋势导致扇出效应减弱，使得估计的长期关系不稳健，这提醒研究者在类似场景下需谨慎解释结果。

Abstract: Many empirical investigations of long-run relations are based on cross-section regressions in averaged or long differenced data that effectively have the time dimension of a $T\times N$ panel compressed. We analyze a class of time-compressed I(1) data and show that they have magnified variability stemming from the fact that the cross-section variance of a non-stationary panel `fans out' with time. Cross-section regressions in time compressed data can potentially yield estimates that are super-consistent and asymptotically normal, whether the regressors are stationary, non-stationary, or highly persistent. The fastest convergence rate of $\sqrt{N}T$ requires a compression scheme that not only magnifies the non-stationary signal, but also dilutes the regression noise. Omitted fixed effects preclude noise dilution but the estimates remain super-consistent. However, the fanning out effect can be weakened when the data have a strong force for mean-reversion or convergence, a problem that seems relevant for temperature data. We consider three applications and find that the long-run relation between consumption and income, and between growth/inflation and demographic variables are reasonably well determined, but the estimated relation between growth and warming temperature is fragile.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [Scalable Self-Supervised Learning for Multiphase AC-OPF in Distribution Systems with Topology Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25095)
*Hoang T. Nguyen,Shaohui Liu,Reetam Sen Biswas,Varsha Pendyala,Nurali Virani,Deepjyoti Deka,Priya L. Donti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出Penalty + Sequential Linearized Feasibility Seeking (SLFS)算法，这是一个针对多相配电系统、可应对拓扑重构的AC-OPF自监督学习框架。该算法无需标注最优解，直接从目标函数和约束训练，利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury更新处理拓扑变化，在13到8500节点的IEEE馈线上实现近零最优性间隙和约束违反，相比IPOPT快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源资源在配电网络的普及要求快速求解多相AC-OPF，以应对变化的负载、DER可用性和拓扑重构。传统非线性求解器速度慢、难以规模化；现有基于学习的方法主要针对平衡输电系统，无法扩展到多相、非平衡且可重构的配电网馈线场景。

Method: Penalty+SLFS采用自监督学习框架，无需标注最优解，直接通过可微的不动点潮流求解器从AC-OPF目标和约束训练。拓扑变化通过Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式高效更新导纳矩阵逆；采用M步Jacobian近似加速通过潮流求解器的微分；推理阶段使用SLFS修复不可行预测以提供可行性保证。

Result: 在IEEE馈线（13至8500节点）上，Penalty+SLFS实现可忽略的最优性间隙和近零约束违反，相比IPOPT快高达三个数量级，并在大规模分布偏移下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Penalty+SLFS展示了实现大规模配电网实时、拓扑感知AC-OPF的可行路径，为配电系统优化提供了高效且稳健的学习方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of distributed energy resources (DERs) in distribution grids enables the active coordination of these assets to reduce costs and enable cleaner operations. Realizing this potential requires solving multiphase AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) quickly across varying loads, DER availabilities, and topology reconfigurations, at much greater speed and scale than conventional nonlinear solvers. Learning-based surrogates can offer millisecond inference, yet existing methods target largely balanced transmission systems and do not scale to the multiphase, unbalanced, and reconfigurable nature of distribution feeders at utility scale. We present the Penalty + Sequential Linearized Feasibility Seeking (SLFS) algorithm, a self-supervised learning framework for multiphase distribution AC-OPF under switch-induced topology changes. Penalty+SLFS requires no labeled optimal solutions and trains directly from the AC-OPF objective and constraints through a differentiable fixed-point power flow solver, avoiding expensive label generation and admitting robust training procedures. Topology changes are handled efficiently using Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury updates of the admittance-matrix inverse, while an M-step Jacobian approximation accelerates differentiation through the power flow solver. At inference, SLFS repairs any infeasible predictions, providing feasibility guarantees with low computational overhead. On IEEE feeders ranging from 13 to 8,500 nodes, Penalty+SLFS achieves negligible optimality gaps and near-zero constraint violations, delivers up to three orders of magnitude speedups over IPOPT, and remains robust under large distributional shifts, demonstrating a viable path toward real-time, topology-aware AC-OPF for large-scale distribution grids.

</details>


### [252] [Compiling Spatial Certificates into Temporal Contracts for Latency-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25228)
*Avinash malik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出CIPS，一种面向安全关键信息物理系统(CPS)的契约驱动执行抽象，通过将异构空间安全证书离线编译为归一化的单位速率时间契约，将复杂的植物动力学抽象为确定性O(1)时间预算，从而在计算延迟和异步执行下保证全局混合安全不变性，在自动驾驶刹车基准测试中相比LA-ETC实现了60.5倍的微架构评估开销降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时控制中的一个根本性挑战是：物理安全证书在空间上定义，但在非零计算和交接延迟下预测其有效性需要在线数值积分植物动力学。这种空间与时间之间的操作二象性造成了管理计算延迟与采样数据更新的困难。

Method: CIPS将异构的空间安全证书系统地编译为归一化的、单位速率的时间契约，这一转换完全离线完成。该抽象掩盖了复杂的植物动力学，向通用采样数据调度器暴露确定性的O(1)时间预算，并形式化证明该架构在有限计算延迟和异步执行下保证全局混合安全不变性。

Result: 通过自动驾驶车辆刹车基准测试验证，相比延迟感知事件触发控制(LA-ETC)，CIPS在保持安全边界的同时实现了60.5倍的微架构评估开销削减。

Conclusion: CIPS通过离线编译时空契约的方式有效解决了CPS中空间安全证书与时间执行之间的冲突，为安全关键实时系统提供了低成本、可证明安全保证的执行抽象框架。

Abstract: We introduce CIPS, a contract-driven execution abstraction for
  managing computational latency and sampled-data updates in
  safety-critical cyber-physical systems (CPS). A fundamental challenge
  in real-time control is that physical safety certificates are defined
  spatially, yet predicting their validity under non-zero computation
  and handoff latency requires online numerical integration of plant
  dynamics. CIPS resolves this operational dichotomy by systematically
  compiling heterogeneous spatial safety certificates into normalized,
  unit-rate temporal contracts entirely offline. This transformation
  abstracts complex plant dynamics, exposing a deterministic, O(1)
  temporal budget to a generic sampled-data scheduler. We formally prove
  that this architecture guarantees global hybrid safety invariance
  under bounded computational latency and asynchronous execution.
  Finally, we validate the framework via an autonomous vehicle braking
  benchmark, demonstrating a 60.5 times reduction in micro-architectural
  evaluation overhead compared to latency-aware event-triggered control
  (LA-ETC) while preserving safety bounds.

</details>


### [253] [Multi-View Trust Evaluation for Collaborator Selection via Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25235)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多视角证据学习（MVE）的分布式系统中合作者信任评估方法，通过将每个任务拥有者视为独立观测视角、利用Mamba模型捕捉时序信任模式、结合证据深度学习量化不确定性，并采用动态证据融合策略整合多视角信息，以提高信任评估准确性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统中选择可信赖的合作者至关重要，但合作者在不同设备、不同场景下的信任数据具有多源、异构、质量不均的特点，导致准确的信任评估成为重大挑战。

Method: 1) 将每个与潜在合作者交互过的任务拥有者建模为独立观测视角，评估视角特定的信任；2) 利用Mamba模型的长序列建模能力捕捉每个视角内信任状态的深度时序模式；3) 引入证据深度学习机制，在输出信任评估结果的同时量化主观不确定性；4) 采用动态证据融合策略，基于量化的不确定性自适应整合多视角证据，得出最终信任评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的MVE方法在信任评估准确性和任务成功率两方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MVE方法通过多视角建模和证据学习有效解决了分布式系统中信任数据多源异构和动态演化的问题，在信任评估准确性和任务成功率上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Selection of trustworthy collaborators in distributed systems is critical for efficient task completion, necessitating the inference of trustworthiness from their past collaboration experience. However, as a collaborator serves distinct devices across diverse scenarios in past collaborations, its trust-related data, observed from different device-specific views, is inherently multi-source, heterogeneous, and uneven in quality. Consequently, achieving accurate trust evaluations for collaborator selection remains a major challenge. To tackle these issues, we propose a novel multi-view evidential learning (MVE) based trust evaluation method. First, to accommodate the multi-source heterogeneity of observed trust-related data, we model each task owner who has interacted with a potential collaborator as an independent observational view, enabling the evaluation of the collaborator's view-specific trust. Second, to address the dynamic evolution of trust under changing conditions, we leverage the powerful long-sequence modeling capability of the Mamba model to capture the deep temporal patterns of a collaborator's trust state within each view. Furthermore, to quantify the certainty levels of view-specific trust assessments, we incorporate an evidential deep learning mechanism in MVE, which outputs trust evaluation results while quantifying the subjective uncertainty underlying them. Finally, we employ a dynamic evidential fusion strategy to adaptively integrate the multi-view evidence based on their respective quantified uncertainties, thereby yielding a final trust evaluation for the collaborator. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed MVE method outperforms baselines in both trust evaluation accuracy and task success rate.

</details>


### [254] [TrustFormer: Cross-Temporal and Cross- Dimensional Transformer for Task-Specific Multi-Dimensional Trust Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25238)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustFormer，一个面向动态协作系统的任务特定多维信任评估框架。它利用任务标识符和设备时间戳同步异构信任数据，通过跨时间和跨维度注意力机制联合建模时间动态与维度间相关性，学习潜在协作者的多维信任演化，并基于任务的多维资源需求选择最优协作者。实验显示相比现有方法，信任评估准确率提升40.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信任评估方法多采用单维或标量表示，无法真实捕捉协作者的可信度。同时，多维信任数据存在异步性问题以及维度内外的复杂依赖关系，使得多维信任评估面临挑战。

Method: TrustFormer框架包含：(1) 利用任务标识符和设备时间戳同步历史协作中的异构信任数据；(2) 采用跨时间和跨维度注意力机制联合建模时间动态与维度间相关性，学习协作者的多维信任演化；(3) 根据任务的多维资源需求，评估协作者各维度的资源信任；(4) 综合多维信任画像，实现最优协作者选择。

Result: 实验结果表明，TrustFormer在信任评估准确率上较现有方法提升40.8%，并能实现更可靠的协作者选择。

Conclusion: TrustFormer通过多维度和跨时间的联合建模，有效解决了动态协作系统中多维信任评估的挑战，显著提升了协作者选择的可靠性和任务执行的有效性。

Abstract: In dynamic collaborative systems, the selection of reliable collaborators is critical to ensuring effective task execution. Existing trust evaluation methods often rely on unidimensional or scalar representations, which fail to faithfully capture a collaborator's true trustworthiness, thereby motivating a shift toward multi-dimensional trust modeling. However, due to the asynchrony of collected trust-related data across different dimensions, as well as the complex intra- and inter-dimensional dependencies embedded within these data, multi-dimensional trust evaluation remains challenging. To address these challenges, we propose TrustFormer, a task-specific multi-dimensional trust evaluation framework. Specifically, TrustFormer leverages task identifiers and device-generated timestamps to synchronize heterogeneous trust-related data across historical collaborations. It further employs cross-temporal and cross-dimensional attention mechanisms to jointly model temporal dynamics and inter-dimensional correlations, thereby effectively learning the multi-dimensional trust evolution of potential collaborators from historical performance data. In addition, according to the multi-dimensional resource requirements of tasks, potential collaborators' multi-dimensional resource trust is evaluated. Finally, by synthesizing these multi-dimensional trust profiles, the framework enables the optimal collaborator selection. Experimental results demonstrate that TrustFormer outperforms existing methods by yielding a 40.8% improvement in trust evaluation accuracy and enabling more reliable collaborator selection.

</details>


### [255] [Integrating Fast-response Capability into Virtual Power Plant Operation for Ancillary Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25247)
*Qixing Liu,Ruike Lyu,Zhe Zhai,Yan Shen,Xue Liu,Hongye Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对虚拟电厂（VPP）提供辅助服务时忽略快速响应能力要求的问题，提出将快速响应能力整合进VPP运营框架，利用历史控制指令构建投标模型中的机会约束，确保VPP的快速响应能力以指定概率满足辅助服务要求。案例研究验证了考虑快速响应能力可提升VPP运营效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟电厂聚合分布式能源提供辅助服务时，如二次调频等辅助服务要求提供商具备足够的快速响应能力以跟踪快速变化的控制指令。若忽视这一响应要求，VPP将无法准确评估其调节能力，导致在基于性能的市场中收益降低或面临取消资格的风险。

Method: 本文将快速响应能力要求整合到VPP提供辅助服务的运营框架中。具体方法为：利用历史控制命令在投标模型中构建机会约束，强制保证VPP的快速响应能力以指定概率满足辅助服务的要求。

Result: 案例研究验证了考虑快速响应能力可以增强VPP的运营，表明将快速响应能力纳入运营框架对VPP有积极效益。

Conclusion: 在VPP投标模型中纳入快速响应能力的机会约束，能够更准确地反映其调节能力，提升在基于性能的辅助服务市场中的竞争力与运营效益。

Abstract: Virtual power plants (VPPs) can aggregate distributed energy resources (DERs) to provide ancillary services for power systems, creating new profit opportunities. Ancillary services such as secondary frequency regulation require providers to have sufficient response capability to follow rapidly changing control commands. If overlooking the response requirement, the VPP will not be able to accurately measure its regulation capability, reducing its earnings in performance-based markets or risking disqualification. This paper integrates the requirement for fast-response capability into the operational framework of VPPs providing ancillary services. We leverage historical control commands to formulate chance constraints in the bidding model, mandating that the VPP's fast-response capability meets the requirement of ancillary services with a specified probability. Case studies verify that considering fast-response capabilities can enhance VPP operation.

</details>


### [256] [LSTN: A Linear Model of Industrial Production Process for Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25249)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Yuanjie Zheng,Yunlong Bai,Qixin Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种工业生产过程线性模型，用于需求响应（DR）应用中的运行约束建模。该模型避免了传统方法中引入二进制变量表示设备离散运行点所带来的计算困难，在保持建模精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业生产过程建模使用二进制变量来模拟设备的离散运行点，在大规模需求响应应用中会导致计算不可行。因此需要在计算复杂度和建模精度之间取得平衡，开发更高效的线性模型。

Method: 提出一种工业生产过程的线性模型，用于评估需求响应应用。该模型替代了传统含二进制变量的混合整数建模方法，通过线性化方式描述工业设备的运行约束，在保证建模准确性的同时降低计算复杂度。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提模型的准确性，与竞争方法相比，在计算效率上实现了大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该线性模型能够在需求响应应用中合理建模工业用户的运行约束，兼顾建模精度与计算效率，为大规模工业DR应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial production modeling provides operational constraints for industrial users participating in demand response (DR) programs. Conventional modeling of the production process introduces binary variables to model the discrete operating points of industrial equipment, which can be computationally infeasible in large-scale DR applications. To reasonably model industrial users' operational constraints while balancing computational complexity and modeling accuracy, we developed a linear model of the industrial production process for evaluating DR applications. Numerical results verify the accuracy of the proposed model and its great improvement in computational efficiency over competing approaches.

</details>


### [257] [Dynamic droop specifications for Grid-Forming Inverter-Based Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25250)
*Jennifer T. Bui,Dominic Groß,Deepak Ramasubramanian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的数据驱动动态模型（动态下垂模型）来刻画逆变器资源（IBR）的小信号动态特性，将传统稳态下垂系数扩展为动态下垂系数，并为其增益和相位设定界以规范构网型（GFM）IBR的最低要求，同时为频率控制辅助服务提供规范，并展示了该工具在筛查IBR动态和潜在不良交互方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着同步发电机大规模退役，电网稳定性和可靠性越来越依赖逆变器资源（IBRs）。由于IBR控制方式异构，理解其电网行为至关重要，但目前缺乏统一的动态模型和规范来评估和认证GFM IBR，尤其是子同步振荡等小信号动态行为。

Method: 提出动态下垂模型，将稳态下垂系数推广为动态下垂系数，以完整表征低于额定工频（如次同步振荡）的IBR小信号响应；在此基础上对动态下垂系数的增益和相位提出边界条件，用于编码GFM IBR的最低要求，并扩展到频率控制辅助服务的规范（推广惯性响应概念）。

Result: 通过常见的跟网型（GFL）和构网型（GFM）控制以及原始设备制造商（OEM）模型进行验证，展示了动态下垂系数作为筛查IBR动态和潜在不良交互工具的有效性，并提供了关于如何认证IBR为GFM的见解。

Conclusion: 动态下垂模型是对阻抗模型的补充，为GFM IBR提供了一套可操作的动态规范，促进互操作性并最小化不良交互，同时澄清和推广了IBR惯性响应的概念，为GFM认证提供了新视角。

Abstract: The large-scale retirement of synchronous generators requires additional capabilities from inverter-based resources (IBRs) to ensure the stability and reliability of power grids. With the heterogeneous controls of IBRs, it is especially important to understand their behavior on the grid. This work proposes a simple data-enabled dynamic model to capture the small-signal dynamics of IBRs and formulate specifications for grid-forming (GFM) IBRs. The dynamic droop model is complementary to well-studied impedance models and extends the common definition of steady-state droop coefficients to dynamic droop coefficients that fully characterize the IBR small-signal response below the nominal line frequency (e.g., subsynchronous oscillations). We propose bounds on the gain and phase of the dynamic droop coefficients to encode minimum requirements for GFM IBRs to promote interoperability and minimize adverse interactions. The resulting specifications also provide some insights into the much-debated question of how to certify an IBR as GFM. Moreover, we also provide dynamic droop specifications for frequency control ancillary services that, e.g., clarify and generalize the notion of an IBR inertia response. Finally, common grid-following (GFL) and GFM controls as well as original equipment manufacturer (OEM) models are used to illustrate the results and showcase the use of dynamic droop coefficients as a tool to screen IBR dynamics for potential adverse interactions.

</details>


### [258] [Analysis of Dynamic-Key LWE-Based Encrypted Control Systems for Asymptotic Stability and Numerical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25255)
*Jungjin Park,Kiminao Kogiso*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于动态密钥学习误差（LWE）的加密状态反馈控制系统，该系统采用时变编码器和解码器。通过Lyapunov方法，推导了时变编解码器参数的条件，确保系统渐近稳定并防止数值溢出，最后通过数值示例验证了条件的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE加密控制系统中，动态密钥（时变编码器和解码器）会引入时变的加密噪声，这对系统的稳定性和数值安全性带来挑战。需要研究如何设计编解码器参数以保证系统在加密环境下既能保持渐近稳定，又能防止数值溢出（即保证数值安全性）。

Method: 采用基于Lyapunov的方法，对动态密钥LWE加密状态反馈控制系统进行分析。通过构造Lyapunov函数，推导出时变编码器和解码器参数需要满足的条件，这些条件同时保证系统的渐近稳定性和数值安全性（防止溢出）。

Result: 推导出了时变编解码器参数的条件，确保加密控制系统的渐近稳定性和数值安全性（防止溢出），并通过数值示例验证了所推导条件的有效性和正确性。

Conclusion: 基于Lyapunov方法的分析表明，通过合理设计时变编码器和解码器参数，可以同时保证LWE加密状态反馈控制系统的渐近稳定性和数值安全性，为动态密钥加密控制系统的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This study analyzes dynamic-key Learning-with-Errors (LWE)-based encrypted state-feedback control systems with time-varying encoders and decoders. Using a Lyapunov-based approach, we derive conditions on the time-varying encoder and decoder parameters that ensure both asymptotic stability and numerical safety by preventing overflow. The validity of the derived conditions is confirmed through numerical examples.

</details>


### [259] [Observability Blocking in a Linear Synchronization Network with Partial State Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25259)
*Alexis Moreno,Abdullah Al Maruf*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模网络系统中，攻击者可通过少量被攻破节点推断系统动态的隐患，提出了在有限状态信息条件下阻断此类推断的多种控制策略。方法包括基于输出反馈、基于观测器以及分布式框架的控制设计，在保留开放环特征值的同时实现可观性阻断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的状态反馈方法虽能实现可观性阻断并保留特征值，但需要全状态访问，在大规模网络中不切实际。因此需要研究在部分状态测量条件下仍然有效的控制策略。

Method: 提出三种控制策略：(1) 输出反馈方法，实现可观性阻断同时保留部分开放环特征值；(2) 基于观测器重构系统状态并实现全状态反馈控制，保留全部特征值且传感器布置更灵活；(3) 将观测器设计扩展到分布式框架。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了所提方法的有效性与适用范围。

Conclusion: 基于观测器的方法在保留全部特征值和传感器布置灵活性方面优于输出反馈方法，分布式框架进一步提升了在大规模系统中的适用性。

Abstract: Large-scale networked systems are increasingly vulnerable to adversaries that can infer system dynamics from a small set of compromised nodes. This paper addresses the problem of blocking such inference using limited state information. While existing state-feedback methods achieve observability blocking with eigenvalue preservation, they require full state access and are impractical for large networks. We propose multiple control strategies that operate under partial state measurements. The first approach employs output feedback to achieve observability blocking while preserving a subset of open-loop eigenvalues. The second approach leverages an observer to reconstruct the system state and enables full-state feedback control, preserving all eigenvalues and providing greater flexibility in sensor placement. We further extend the observer-based design to a distributed framework. Numerical examples demonstrate the scope and validity of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [260] [SonicNudge: Controlled Displacement of Hovering UAVs via Estimator-Controller Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25319)
*Shaocheng Luo,Ashir Raza,Haocheng Meng,David Hunt,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出SonicNudge，一种新型UAV攻击原语，通过超声谐振对陀螺仪施加微小扰动，利用估计器-控制器耦合，将低层级惯性误差转化为无人机悬停或慢速移动时的受控位置位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UAV位移攻击主要依赖欺骗直接报告位置或平移运动的传感器（如GNSS、光流），而陀螺仪这类惯性传感器作为攻击面未被充分研究。作者希望揭示低层级惯性误差经估计器-控制器耦合放大后，同样能造成具有作战意义的位移。

Method: 攻击利用超声谐振对陀螺仪产生微小扰动，该扰动可在姿态估计中形成持续偏差；飞行控制器将这一偏差估计转换为偏移的悬停点。作者在PX4风格飞行栈中分析该攻击原语，并通过81次仿真和10余次室内外物理实验进行验证。

Result: 研究表明位移受估计器权重、偏差可观测性和闭环位置修正共同支配。攻击在悬停、定点保持、慢速接近、精确对准等任务场景中可产生米级位置误差，具有作战意义。

Conclusion: UAV及车辆系统安全应超越直接导航欺骗的视角，关注低层级惯性误差和估计器-控制器耦合这一隐蔽但重要的攻击面。

Abstract: UAV displacement attacks have traditionally relied on spoofing sensors that directly report position or translational motion, such as GNSS and optical flow. In this work, we introduce SonicNudge, a new attack primitive that instead targets the gyroscope and shows that low-level inertial errors can be transformed into controlled displacement of hovering or slow-moving UAVs. The attack exploits estimator--controller coupling: a small gyroscope perturbation by ultrasonic resonance can persist as an attitude-estimation bias, and the flight controller can convert this biased estimate into a shifted hover point. This behavior is especially relevant to UAV tasks that require hovering, station-keeping, slow approach, or precise final alignment, such as perimeter denial, inspection, docking, landing alignment, and close-proximity operation, where meter-scale position errors can be operationally meaningful. We analyze this attack primitive in a PX4-style flight stack and validate it through 81 simulation runs and more than 10 indoor/outdoor physical experiments, showing that displacement is governed by estimator weighting, bias observability, and closed-loop position correction. Our study suggests that UAV and vehicle-system security should look beyond direct navigation spoofing and pay closer attention to low-level inertial errors and estimator--controller coupling as a subtle but important attack surface.

</details>


### [261] [Scalable Tube-Tightened Multi-Agent Safety via Certified Constraint Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25323)
*Armel Koulong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Farkas证书的约束约减方法，用于多智能体分布式模型预测控制中的管式指数控制障碍函数（eCBFs）安全约束验证与计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体分布式MPC中，每步预测需要验证大量智能体间及智能体与障碍物间的eCBF半空间约束，计算开销随智能体数量和预测时域增长而急剧增加，需要一种能保证安全性且降低计算负担的约束约减方法。

Method: 在局部控制空间中，通过几何自适应机制选择保留少量半空间约束：对平面输入，利用最大角间距刻画锥覆盖，在严格半平面情形下仅需两个极端方向，其他几何情形初始保留三个约束，仅在证书失败时升级。使用Farkas引理验证约减后集合包含于完整收紧集合，锥乘子与标称感知偏移以闭式解获得，无需辅助优化。

Result: 在十追随者、四障碍物的仿真中，该方法平均保留更少的安全约束，完整复现全滤波器的标称接受/拒绝决策，无真实安全违例，并随约束数和预测时域增长获得递增的计算加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束约减控制器继承了底层管式eCBF框架的鲁棒安全保障，在保证安全性的同时显著降低了分布式MPC的计算负担。

Abstract: This paper develops a certified constraint-reduction method for distributed model predictive control with tube-tightened exponential control barrier functions (eCBFs) in multi-agent systems. At each prediction stage, pairwise agent--agent and agent--obstacle eCBF conditions define halfspaces in the local control space. Rather than enforcing all such halfspaces, a geometry-adaptive subset is retained and a Farkas certificate verifies that the reduced admissible set is contained in the full tightened set. For planar inputs, cone coverage is characterized through the largest angular gap: two extreme directions suffice in the strict half-plane regime, while other geometries initialize with three retained constraints and escalate only when certification fails. Conic multipliers and nominal-aware offsets are obtained in closed form, without an auxiliary optimization, and the resulting construction preserves any nominal control already admissible for the full tightened set. Consequently, the reduced controller inherits the robust safety guarantee of the underlying tube-eCBF formulation. In a ten-follower, four-obstacle study, the method retained fewer safety constraints on average, reproduced the full filter's nominal accept/reject decisions with no true safety violations, and achieved increasing computational gains as the constraint count and prediction horizon grew.

</details>


### [262] [Goodput Maximization for Large Language Model Edge Inference: A Two-Phase Maskable PPO Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25543)
*Xiaojing Chen,Qi Zhang,Wei Ni,Shunqing Zhang,Yanzan Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段可掩蔽近端策略优化（TP-MPPO）算法，用于无线边缘网络中LLM推理服务，旨在最大化系统吞吐量同时严格满足SLO合规性，仿真显示系统奖励提升33.3%-87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）推理服务部署于无线边缘网络的场景中，如何在保证服务级别目标（SLO）严格合规的前提下，最大化系统的请求吞吐量（goodput）是一个关键挑战。现有的优化方法在任务卸载决策和带宽分配等方面缺乏高效协同，探索无效动作导致性能受限。

Method: 提出两阶段可掩蔽PPO算法（TP-MPPO）：第一阶段使用带动作掩蔽机制的MPPO优化任务卸载决策，通过掩蔽机制避免无效动作探索并缩小动作空间；第二阶段推导上行链路带宽分配的闭式解，并设计贪心算法进行下行链路带宽分配，为下一轮MPPO提供即时奖励。两个阶段交替进行直至收敛。

Result: 仿真结果表明，TP-MPPO相比基准方法将系统奖励提升了33.3%-87.5%，并实现了最高的goodput（有效吞吐量）。

Conclusion: TP-MPPO通过两阶段交替优化的架构，结合动作掩蔽PPO与闭式解/贪心算法的带宽分配，能够有效提升无线边缘网络中LLM推理服务的吞吐量并保证SLO合规性。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel two-phase maskable proximal policy optimization (TP-MPPO) algorithm, which maximizes the system goodput counting request throughput with strict service level objective (SLO) compliance for large language model (LLM) inference services in wireless edge networks. In the first phase of TP-MPPO, we optimize the task offloading decisions by MPPO with action masking mechanism, effectively avoiding exploring invalid actions and reducing the action space. In the second phase, closed-form solutions are derived for uplink bandwidth allocation; a greedy algorithm is designed for downlink bandwidth allocation to provide immediate rewards for the MPPO in the next round. The two stages alternate till convergence. Simulation results demonstrate that TP-MPPO can improve the system reward by 33.3%--87.5% compared to its benchmarks and achieve the highest goodput.

</details>


### [263] [Throughput Maximization for MapReduce-Based Collaborative Computing over Energy-Harvesting Wireless Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25549)
*Yuhang Li,Siqi Sun,Hongen Zheng,Xiaojing Chen,Shunqing Zhang,Yanzan Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于可再生能源收集的异构无线设备上MapReduce协作计算的资源分配问题，提出了一种将深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）与凸优化相结合的DDPG-CVX在线算法，实现了长期平均吞吐量最大化，仿真表明其吞吐量达到代表性基准的1.25~32.36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可再生能源供电的异构无线设备上运行MapReduce协作计算时，资源分配面临电池动态、信道状态和能量到达未知等挑战，现有方法难以在保证约束的同时在线优化长期吞吐量。

Method: 将长期平均吞吐量最大化问题分解为两阶段：DDPG根据观测到的电池和信道状态确定每设备时隙能量预算；其余资源分配变量（计算负载、相位时间、发射功率等）由内嵌凸优化求解器全局最优求解，从而降低动作空间维度并保持求解质量。

Result: 仿真结果表明，DDPG-CVX算法相比代表性基准方案，吞吐量提升达1.25倍至32.36倍。

Conclusion: 提出的DDPG-CVX算法能够在无信道状态和能量到达先验知识的情况下在线进行资源分配，两阶段分解有效平衡了求解复杂度与最优性，为能量收集边缘计算系统的资源管理提供了高效方案。

Abstract: This paper studies resource allocation for MapReduce-based collaborative computing over heterogeneous wireless devices powered by renewable energy harvesting. We formulate a long-run average throughput maximization problem that jointly optimizes computing load, phase time allocations, transmit power, and per-device energy consumption, subject to battery evolution, CPU frequency, and latency constraints. To solve this problem online without prior knowledge of channel states or energy arrivals, we propose a DDPG-CVX algorithm that couples Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) with convex programming. DDPG determines the per-slot energy budget for each device from observed battery and channel states; the remaining resource allocation variables are then resolved to global optimality by an embedded convex solver. This two-phase decomposition reduces the action-space dimensionality of DDPG while preserving per-slot solution quality. Simulations show that DDPG-CVX achieves 1.25$\times$$\sim$32.36$\times$ the throughput of representative benchmarks.

</details>


### [264] [The Impact of PV Generation Forecast and Multi-Objective Control Policy on Optimal Operation of Grid Connected PV-BESS Microgrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25703)
*Berhane Darsene Dimd,Steve Voller,Ole-Morten Midtgård*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LSTM的光伏功率预测模型，集成到并网光伏-电池储能系统的多目标调度框架中，通过对比完美预测、持久性模型和LSTM预测三种场景，量化了预测精度对系统关键运行指标的影响。结果显示LSTM预测相比持久性模型RMSE降低6%，光伏自消耗率从78.1%提升至84.5%，电网注入量减少82%，但更高的电池吞吐量可能加速电池老化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏发电的波动性给并网微电网的可靠高效运行带来重大挑战，准确的光伏出力预测和高效的能源调度策略对于优化系统运行和提升整体性能至关重要。

Method: 构建基于LSTM的光伏功率预测模型，并将其集成到并网光伏-电池储能系统的多目标调度框架中；通过设置三种预测场景（完美预测、持久性模型、LSTM预测）进行对比评估，考察预测精度对光伏自消耗率、电网购电成本、电网注入量和电池利用率等关键运行指标的影响。

Result: LSTM预测相比持久性模型RMSE降低6%，光伏自消耗率从78.1%提升至84.5%，电网注入量减少82%；同时发现更高电池吞吐量可能加速电池老化，存在性能提升与电池寿命之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 准确的光伏预测对提升系统性能和保障可靠运行至关重要；未来工作将聚焦于概率预测以量化不确定性、纳入负荷预测，以及开发支持光伏侧电网辅助功能的智能控制策略。

Abstract: The variability of photovoltaic (PV) generation poses significant challenges to the reliable and efficient operation of grid-connected microgrids. Accurate PV output power forecasting and efficient energy scheduling strategies are essential not only for optimizing PV system operation but also for improving the overall performance and reliability of the system. This study proposes a long short-term memory (LSTM)-based PV power forecasting model integrated with a multi-objective scheduling framework for a grid-connected PV-battery energy storage system (BESS). The proposed approach enables detailed performance monitoring and assessment by quantifying how PV forecast accuracy influences key operational metrics, including PV self-consumption ratio, grid energy cost, grid injection, and battery utilization. Three forecasting scenarios (perfect forecast, persistence model, and LSTM-based forecast) are compared to evaluate their impact on system performance and operational reliability. Results show that the LSTM-based forecast reduces root mean squared error (RMSE) by 6% compared with the persistence model, increases the PV self-consumption ratio from 78.1% to 84.5%, and reduces grid injections by 82%. The analysis also highlights trade-offs, as higher battery throughput associated with improved performance may contribute to accelerated aging. These findings demonstrate the importance of accurate PV forecasting in improving system performance and ensuring reliable operation. Future work will focus on probabilistic forecasting to properly quantify uncertainties, incorporate load prediction, and develop smart control strategies that allow grid-support functionalities from the PV side.

</details>


### [265] [Model predictive traction control system based on the Koopman operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25753)
*Josip Kir Hromatko,Šandor Ileš*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论和模型预测控制（MPC）的牵引力控制系统。通过状态空间变换将非线性轮胎动力学近似为线性系统，并基于Koopman预测器设计了线性MPC控制器，与标准非线性MPC控制器进行对比。在高保真车辆动力学仿真环境中实验表明，两种控制器具有相当的参考跟踪性能，但所提出的Koopman算子算法在标准PC和嵌入式硬件上均具有更短的执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牵引力控制和防抱死制动系统是现代车辆的标准配置，但轮胎动力学的精确模型难以获得且通常包含非线性，这给控制系统设计带来挑战。传统非线性MPC虽然能处理非线性问题，但计算成本高昂，难以满足实时性要求。因此需要一种既能处理非线性动力学，又具有较低计算复杂度的控制方案。

Method: 利用Koopman算子理论，通过状态空间变换将非线性轮胎动力学系统近似为线性系统；基于该Koopman预测器设计了一个线性模型预测控制器；将该控制器与标准的非线性MPC控制器进行对比实验，在高保真车辆动力学仿真环境中进行评估，并在标准PC和嵌入式硬件上分别测试执行时间。

Result: 在高保真车辆动力学仿真环境中，基于Koopman算子的线性MPC控制器与标准非线性MPC控制器展现出可比的参考跟踪性能；同时在标准PC和嵌入式硬件上，所提出的Koopman算子算法具有更短的执行时间。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子理论的牵引力控制系统能够在保持与非线性MPC相当的跟踪性能的同时，显著降低计算负担和执行时间，使其更适合在嵌入式硬件上实时运行，为车辆牵引力控制提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Due to their importance, traction control and anti-lock braking systems have become standard equipment in modern vehicles. However, accurate models of tire dynamics are often difficult to obtain and usually include nonlinearities, making their use in control systems challenging. This paper describes a traction control system based on model predictive control and Koopman operator theory, which aims to approximate nonlinear systems with linear ones through a state space transformation. A linear model predictive controller based on the Koopman predictor is compared to a standard nonlinear model predictive controller. Experiments in a high-fidelity vehicle dynamics simulation environment show a comparable reference tracking performance of the two controllers, with a reduced execution time for the proposed Koopman operator-based algorithm, both on a standard PC and embedded hardware.

</details>


### [266] [UNION: A Unified AC-OPF Framework for Topology-Varying Real-Time Grid Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25784)
*Kyungnam Park,Keunju Song,Yeji Lim,Suho Park,Kibaek Kim,Hongseok Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出UNION，一个统一的基于图的AC-OPF框架，支持异构系统和拓扑变化运行。通过共享图编码器、标量门控聚合、稀疏感知可微隐式层以及原始-对偶训练和确定性恢复层，单个模型跨七个系统联合训练（包括4,492节点的韩国真实电网），实现1.23%的平均目标差距，99.56%的测试实例满足所有运行约束，并能在零样本N-1故障和多天时变拓扑下保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全实时电网运行需要快速且准确的AC-OPF工具，但现有学习方法大多针对单一系统或单一拓扑训练，且难以保证输出满足所有运行限制。需要一种能够跨异构系统和拓扑变化场景统一工作的框架。

Method: 提出UNION框架，包含：1) 共享图编码器处理异构系统；2) 带显式一致性校正的标量门控聚合；3) 嵌入AC潮流方程的稀疏感知可微隐式层；4) 原始-对偶训练处理剩余不等式约束；5) 确定性恢复层保证可行性。

Result: 单模型跨七个系统联合训练（包括4,492节点韩国电网），平均目标差距1.23%，99.56%测试实例满足所有运行约束；在零样本N-1故障和五天时变韩国拓扑下保持性能；轻量在线微调后以2.51%差距保持全部快照覆盖；预恢复推理55-58ms/实例，含恢复108-114ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一个联合训练、物理一致的模型可以支持跨异构系统和演化拓扑的实时AC-OPF，为电网安全实时运行提供了可行的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Secure real-time grid operation requires fast AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) tools that stay accurate and feasible as operating conditions and topology change. Learning-based methods have advanced, but most are trained per system or per topology, and delivering an operating point that satisfies every operational limit remains challenging. This paper proposes UNION, a unified graph-based AC-OPF framework for heterogeneous systems and topology-varying operation. UNION proposes a shared graph encoder, a scalar-gated aggregation with explicit consensus correction, and a sparse-aware differentiable implicit layer embedding the AC power-flow equations. The remaining inequalities are handled by primal-dual training and the deterministic restoration layer. A single model trained jointly across seven systems, including a real-world 4,492-bus Korean transmission grid, attains a 1.23% mean objective gap and satisfies every operational limit on 99.56% of test instances. It sustains this under zero-shot $N-1$ contingencies, i.e., line and generator outages, and over five days of time-varying Korean topologies; it retains full snapshot coverage at a 2.51% gap under lightweight online fine-tuning. UNION pre-restoration inference takes 55$-$58 ms per instance on the three largest systems, and 108$-$114 ms including restoration. These results indicate that one jointly trained, physics-consistent model can support real-time AC-OPF across heterogeneous systems and evolving topologies.

</details>


### [267] [Hard-Constrained Sampling on Embedded Riemannian Manifolds via Adjoint Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25838)
*Mattia Mosso,Jaemoo Choi,Heng Yang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在光滑紧致连通黎曼流形上从未归一化玻尔兹曼分布采样的方法，基于伴随匹配和伴随薛定谔桥采样的基础，通过随机最优控制的视角提供了理论验证的方法，并保证可行性在状态空间层面（即受控扩散内在定义在弯曲空间上），在多个物理应用上进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多任务需要从流形上支持的未归一化玻尔兹曼分布中采样，这是一个具有挑战性的问题，现有文献中大多数方法没有在流形几何层面确保采样的可行性。

Method: 基于伴随匹配（adjoint matching）和伴随薛定谔桥采样（adjoint Schrödinger bridge sampling）的理论框架，从随机最优控制的角度出发，将受控扩散定义在光滑、紧致、路径连通的嵌入黎曼流形上，从而在状态空间层面强制保证可行性。

Result: 在多个物理应用上提供了实证验证，表明该方法能够有效地在弯曲空间上进行采样。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论和实践上为流形上的玻尔兹曼分布采样提供了有理论保证的解决方案，其新颖之处在于将可行性约束置于状态空间层面，而非仅在数值近似层面。

Abstract: A variety of tasks require sampling from unnormalized Boltzmann distributions supported on manifolds. Building upon the foundations of adjoint matching and adjoint Schrödinger bridge sampling, this paper provides a theoretically justified method, through the lens of stochastic optimal control, to address this problem on smooth, compact, path-connected embedded Riemannian manifolds. As an element of novelty compared to existing literature, feasibility is enforced at the level of the state space, meaning the controlled diffusion is defined intrinsically on the curved space. Empirical validations are provided for several physics applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [The AI Adaptation Gap in Higher Education: Students, Faculty, and Administrative Staff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25063)
*Yuriy S. Braun,Salavat M. Khafizov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了一所大型师范类大学中1809名学生、250名教师和62名行政人员（共2121人）的AI使用模式与态度，使用三种角色适配的75项问卷。结果发现明显的AI适应差距：学生AI使用强度和感知有用性高于教师和行政人员，而教职员工有更强的学术诚信担忧和负责任使用规范认同。感知有用性是对AI信任最强的预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在高等教育中的普及，不同大学群体（学生、教师、行政人员）对AI的使用模式和态度可能存在显著差异，但现有研究缺乏对三者进行系统比较的大规模调查。了解这些差异对于制定差异化政策和支持措施至关重要。

Method: 使用三种角色适配的75项问卷收集数据，涵盖AI使用频率与情境、感知有用性、信任与控制、学术诚信关注、负责任使用规范、机构政策清晰度及输出质量改进感知。分析方法包括描述性统计、Welch组间比较、合并OLS回归模型、信度与维度检验，以及仅针对学生的探索性K-means聚类。

Result: 学生AI使用强度和感知有用性显著高于教师和行政人员；教职员工表现出更强的学术诚信担忧和负责任使用规范认同。在合并OLS信任模型中，感知有用性（beta=0.402）与机构政策清晰度（beta=0.223）均与AI信任正相关。学生感知到的政策清晰度高于教师。学生群体内部存在异质性但未形成跨群体的潜在类型学。

Conclusion: 高等教育中存在显著且结构化的AI适应差距，不同群体在AI使用、感知和规范认同方面存在差异。研究局限在于横断面自报告数据只能揭示关联和组间差异，不能推断因果关系或客观学习结果。

Abstract: The purpose of this study was to analyze patterns of artificial intelligence (AI) use and attitudes toward AI among students, faculty, and administrative staff at a large university specializing in teacher education. The analytical sample comprised 1809 students, 250 faculty members, and 62 administrative staff members (N = 2121). Three role-adapted 75-item questionnaires covered the frequency and contexts of AI use, perceived usefulness, trust and control, academic integrity concerns, responsible-use norms, institutional policy clarity, and perceived improvement in output quality. Data analysis included descriptive statistics, Welch group comparisons, pooled ordinary least squares (OLS) models, reliability and dimensionality checks for observed indices, and exploratory student-only K-means clustering. The results revealed a pronounced AI adaptation gap across university groups. Students reported higher current AI-use intensity and perceived usefulness than faculty and administrative staff, whereas faculty and administrative staff reported stronger academic integrity concerns and greater endorsement of responsible-use norms. In the pooled OLS trust model, perceived usefulness had the strongest standardized positive association with trust in AI (beta = 0.402); institutional policy clarity also had a positive but weaker association (beta = 0.223). Students reported higher perceived policy clarity than faculty, while neither group differed significantly from administrative staff. Exploratory clustering indicated heterogeneity among students in experience, competence, usefulness, trust, and control, but did not establish a latent typology across university groups. The cross-sectional, self-reported data show associations and group differences rather than causal effects on learning or objective outcomes.

</details>


### [269] [Self-Explanation Tutor for Active Study of CS1 Worked Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25180)
*Arun-Balajiee Lekshmi-Narayanan,Mohammad Hassany,Kamil Akhuseyinoglu,Rully Hendrawan,Peter Brusilovsky*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ESSE——一个基于大语言模型(LLM)的入门编程自我解释辅导系统。学生解释工作示例的代码行，系统提供即时反馈。研究发现LLM对解释的评判足够可靠（与专家和众包评分员对照），且该系统能促使学生坚持修改解释、写出更完整丰富的解释，并表现出学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作示例是入门编程教学的重要部分，但阅读专家解释是被动的。自我解释（学生以子目标层面分析主动解释问题和解决方案）能化被动为主动，但评估自由文本解释并返回及时反馈缺乏自动化解决方案。作者探究LLM能否填补这一空白。

Method: 构建ESSE自我解释辅导系统：学生解释工作示例的代码行，LLM即时反馈解释的正确性和完整性。通过两个独立人类参考标准（单一领域专家和非专家众包评分员）评估LLM评判质量；并在入门Java课程中部署，考察反馈对学生坚持性、解释质量及学习效果的影响。

Result: LLM评判与人类参考标准总体吻合，可靠性与系统偏差特征被刻画；实际部署中，LLM反馈促使学生坚持并修订而非放弃某行解释，学生解释在多次尝试中变得更完整、概念更丰富，且表现出学习证据。

Conclusion: LLM为基础的评估足以支撑自我解释辅导系统，该导师能积极塑造学生学习工作示例的方式。

Abstract: Worked examples are a important part of introductory programming, but reading their expert explanations is passive. Self explanation, students explaining the problem and its solution to themselves with subgoal level analysis, turns that study into an active task, yet it is hard to scale because assessing free-text explanations and returning timely feedback has had no easy automated solution. We investigate whether a large language model (LLM) can fill that gap. We build a self-explanation tutor for introductory programming, ESSE, in which students explain lines of worked examples and receive immediate LLM feedback on the correctness and completeness of each explanation, and we pursue two goals. First, we ask whether the LLM judges student explanations well enough to serve as the engine of the tutor; we assess its judgments against two independent human reference standards of different kinds, a single domain expert and a crowd of non-expert raters, each with its own strengths and weaknesses, characterizing both where the LLM is reliable and the systematic tendencies in how it diverges. Second, we ask whether the LLM-based tutoring benefits students; deploying it in an introductory Java course, we find that its feedback leads students to persist and revise rather than abandon a line, that their explanations grow more complete and conceptually richer across attempts, and that students show evidence of learning. These indicate that LLM-based assessment is good enough to power a self-explanation tutor, and that the tutor positively shapes how students study worked examples.

</details>


### [270] [Rare Diseases, Common Dilemmas: LLMs Prioritize Equal Resource Distribution over Patient Benefit in Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25236)
*Minda Zhao,Xu Han,Rishabh Goel,Maya Dagan,Noa Dagan,Adithya Madduri,Payal Chandak,Shilpa Nadimpalli Kobren,Isaac S. Kohane*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含208个临床罕见病场景的基准测试，评估11个最先进LLM在伦理冲突情境下的临床决策。研究发现所有模型一致优先考虑公正原则而非其他生物伦理原则，且存在强烈的权威框架效应——决策者身份会影响模型的伦理优先级选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病护理场景中伦理冲突普遍存在，且稀缺的先验信息可能影响LLM行为，但已有研究缺乏对这一领域的LLM决策评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索LLM在罕见病高层级伦理冲突中的决策倾向。

Method: 构建208个临床依据的罕见病例场景基准，每个场景呈现真实临床可行但在伦理上冲突的下一步选择。对11个最先进LLM进行提示，让其在伦理冲突选项中做选择，并分析不同框架（委员会、临床医生、患者）下的决策差异。

Result: 所有被测试的LLM都一致优先考虑公正原则而非其他核心生物伦理原则；模型压倒性地偏好平等资源分配而非基于需求的考量；存在强烈的权威框架效应，决策由委员会、临床医生或患者做出时，模型分别偏向公正、行善和自主原则。

Conclusion: LLM的决策可能无意识地反映了围绕罕见病资源利用的制度压力，而更精细的伦理考量被忽视。这提醒在将LLM整合到临床决策支持系统时需要谨慎，并需关注其潜在的伦理偏见。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making often involves prioritizing ethical values, such as beneficence, non-maleficence, respecting a patient's autonomy, and justice. Recent work has begun to assess how large language models (LLMs) make such subjective, value-laden clinical judgments. However, evaluations of LLM decision-making in rare disease care contexts, where ethical tensions are ubiquitous and where scarce prior information likely impacts LLM behavior, are still lacking. Here, we present a benchmark of 208 clinically grounded rare disease vignettes, each of which presents genuine, high-stakes conflicts. When prompting 11 state-of-the-art LLMs to choose between clinically defensible yet ethically conflicting next steps embedded within these vignettes, we found that all evaluated models consistently prioritized justice over other core bioethical principles. Specifically, models overwhelmingly favor equal resource allocation over need-based considerations, indicating LLMs' limited responsiveness to differences in clinical severity or situational context. We also identify a strong authority-framing effect: models favor justice in committee-based contexts and shift toward beneficence and autonomy only when final decisions are framed as being made by clinicians or patients respectively. Our work suggests that institutional pressures surrounding rare disease resource utilization may be silently reflected in LLM-based decision support systems, with finer ethical considerations disregarded.

</details>


### [271] [Framing War Across Languages: Power, Agency, and Sentiment in Wikipedia's Multilingual War Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25337)
*Jiarui Xia,Diego Gomez-Zara*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究分析了1900年以来158场战争在20种维基百科语言版本中的描述差异，利用内涵框架（connotation frames）评估交战方的权力、能动性和情感倾向。研究发现，当语言版本描述涉及自身社区的战争时存在系统性差异（但方向因语言而异），而描述与自身无关的冲突时各语言版本高度相似，揭示语言社区对战争叙事的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科宣称对历史冲突持中立视角，但不同语言版本由来自不同语言和文化背景的编辑撰写。此前缺乏系统性地跨语言比较来检验语言社区是否以及如何影响战争叙事的客观性。

Method: 选取1900年以来158场战争，覆盖20种维基百科语言版本。采用内涵框架（connotation frames）方法，从权力（power）、能动性（agency）和情感（sentiment）三个维度评估各语言版本对交战方的描述，并区分涉及自身语言社区与不涉及自身社区的战争进行对比分析。

Result: 发现语言版本在描述涉及自身社区的战争时存在系统性差异，但不对称的方向因语言而异；而描述不涉及自身社区的冲突时，各语言版本的叙事结构高度相似。

Conclusion: 语言社区显著影响维基百科上的战争叙事。即使维基百科标榜中立视角，共享的历史叙述仍受到产出它们的语言社区视角的塑造，挑战了跨语言维基百科内容完全中立的假设。

Abstract: While Wikipedia promotes a neutral point of view on historical conflicts, its language editions are written by editors from distinct linguistic and cultural communities. In this study, we analyze 158 wars since 1900 to examine how the descriptions of combatants vary across 20 Wikipedia language editions. Using connotation frames---which assess power, agency, and sentiment toward an entity---we examine how each language portrays the parties involved in the conflict. We find systematic differences when language editions describe wars involving their own communities, although the direction of these asymmetries varies across languages. However, when language editions describe conflicts that do not involve their own linguistic communities, their narrative structures exhibit high cross-linguistic similarity. These findings show how linguistic communities influence war narratives on Wikipedia, revealing that shared historical accounts remain shaped by the perspectives of the language communities that produce them.

</details>


### [272] [Toward a Threat Actor Profiling Taxonomy for Pre-Release Risk Management of Open-Weight Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25361)
*James Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将显式对手特征描述视为前沿AI滥用风险评估的前提条件，并提出了一个六属性分类法（技术熟练度、领域知识、组织能力、运营基础设施、财务能力和时间范围），为预发布风险评估提供通用的对手假设描述语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI发布前的滥用风险管理中，威胁行为者假设常常是隐式的、不一致的或缺乏依据的，导致评估难以解释、比较和忠实反映实际风险。作者认为明确的对手特征描述应被视为可信评估的前提条件。

Method: 提出六属性分类法，涵盖技术熟练度、领域知识、组织能力、运营基础设施、财务能力及时间范围，并将层级建立在现有恐怖主义、生物安全和网络安全文献的基础上（经验驱动），作为类似于医学和经济学RCT预分析计划的研究基础设施。

Result: 构建了一个用于在评估前预先指定对手假设的通用分类框架，特别强调了开源权重模型开发者面临的不可逆发布决策需要预见对抗性推理的紧迫性。

Conclusion: 对手特征的系统性描述应成为AI预发布风险评估的标准组成部分，尤其对开源权重模型开发者具有紧迫的应用价值。

Abstract: Pre-release risk management for frontier AI misuse risks routinely leaves threat actor assumptions implicit, inconsistently specified, or ungrounded. This capstone argues that explicit adversary characterization should be regarded as a prerequisite for evaluations that are interpretable, comparable, and faithful to the risks they target. We propose a six-attribute taxonomy (covering technical sophistication, prior domain knowledge, organizational capacity, operational infrastructure, financial capacity, and time horizon) with empirically grounded tiers derived from existing terrorism, biosecurity, and cybersecurity literature. The taxonomy is designed to function as research infrastructure: a common language for pre-specifying adversary assumptions before evaluations are conducted, analogous to pre-analysis plans for randomized controlled trials (RCTs) in medicine and economics. Its application is particularly urgent for open-weight model developers, for whom release decisions are irreversible and must anticipate adversarial reasoning.

</details>


### [273] [GGSS: Geodesic-Gated Spherical Steering for Inference-Time Debiasing of Generative Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25375)
*Yiqun Sun,Junyu Chen,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出GGSS（测地线门控球面转向），一种针对生成式视觉-语言模型的推断时去偏方法，通过在单位超球面上发现反事实偏置子空间、沿测地线弧转向视觉token，并用自适应门控聚焦高人口统计信号的token，在四个生成式VLM上取得最低平均偏置且保持MMStar精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉-语言模型在人类中心场景中可能产生人口统计偏置（如种族、性别），但现有推断时去偏器主要针对静态嵌入或CLIP类模型设计，不适用于生成式VLM，缺乏有效的推断时偏置纠正方案。

Method: GGSS包含三个核心组件：(1) 在单位超球面上发现反事实偏置子空间（norm-preserving）；(2) 使视觉token沿测地线弧转向以纠正偏置方向；(3) 使用自适应门控，仅对携带更强人口统计信号的token施加更强校正。评估采用单一操作点协议，涵盖分类、成对和职业-性别偏置测试。

Result: 在四个生成式VLM上，GGSS实现了所有模型的最低平均偏置，其中三个骨干网络在配对置换检验下显著优于基线；同时MMStar准确率相对未转向基线仅波动±0.6个百分点，保持了通用视觉-语言能力。

Conclusion: GGSS证明了一种保范数、基于球面几何的推断时干预方法能够有效降低生成式VLM的人口统计偏置，且对模型通用能力影响极小，为生成式多模态模型的公平性干预提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in human-centered settings, yet they can produce demographically biased outputs even when images differ only in controlled attributes such as perceived race or gender. However, existing inference-time debiasers were largely designed for static embeddings or CLIP-like models rather than generative VLMs. We propose GGSS---Geodesic-Gated Spherical Steering---a norm-preserving intervention that discovers a counterfactual bias subspace on the unit hypersphere, steers visual tokens along geodesic arcs, and uses an adaptive gate to focus correction on tokens that carry stronger demographic signal. We evaluate four generative VLMs against ten adapted inference-time debiasing baselines and prompt-based mitigation under a single operating-point protocol across categorical, pairwise, and occupation-gender bias tests, while also measuring general visual-language capability. GGSS achieves the lowest average bias on all four models, significant on three of four backbones under paired permutation tests, while preserving MMStar accuracy within +/- 0.6 p.p. of the unsteered baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dukesun99/GGSS.

</details>


### [274] [CompanionHarm: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Detecting Harms in Real-World AI Companion Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25377)
*Renwen Zhang,Han Meng,Jian Chai,Yuntao Lin,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CompanionHarm，一个包含 2,111 段真实世界多轮对话（14,051 条话语）的公开基准数据集，涉及用户与 AI 伴侣 Replika 的交互，其中 7,016 条 AI 话语由三位标注者根据 13 类危害行为分类进行独立标注。评估七个 LLM 后发现，使用多轮对话上下文检测危害优于孤立话语检测，但当前 LLM 仍难以持续整合上下文线索、校准危害严重程度和解读关系边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 伴侣融入日常生活，需要检测社会性和情感性人机交互中出现的危害，但现有研究缺乏真实世界、多轮对话的数据集来操作化和评估具有关系性和情境性的危害。

Method: 构建 CompanionHarm 数据集：收集用户与 Replika 的真实多轮对话，由三位标注者依据基于 AI 伴侣危害分类学的 13 类有害行为进行独立标注，同时提供聚合标签和标注者级标签；并评估七个 LLM 在该数据集上的危害检测性能。

Result: 多轮对话上下文的危害检测优于基于孤立话语的检测；但当前 LLM 在整合情境线索、校准危害严重程度和解读关系边界方面仍存在困难。标注者间分歧显著，尤其对依赖语境的有害行为，分歧程度与标注者的政治倾向、对话长度和话语位置有关。

Conclusion: CompanionHarm 为多轮人机对话中的社会情感危害检测提供了基础，也为严格研究人机对危害的不同解读提供了支撑。

Abstract: As AI companions become increasingly embedded in everyday life, there is an urgent need to detect harms that emerge in social and emotional human-AI interactions. Yet research in this area is constrained by the lack of real-world, multi-turn conversational datasets for operationalizing and evaluating harms that are relational and contextual. In this work, we introduce CompanionHarm, a publicly available benchmark dataset comprising 2,111 real-world, multi-turn conversations (14,051 utterances) between users and the AI companion Replika. 7,016 AI utterances were annotated independently by three annotators across 13 harmful behavior categories grounded in a taxonomy of AI companion harms, and the dataset includes both aggregated labels and annotator-level labels to support model evaluation and systematic disagreement analysis. Evaluations of seven large language models (LLMs) show that harm detection using multi-turn conversational context outperforms detection based on isolated utterances, although current LLMs still struggle to consistently integrate contextual cues, calibrate harm severity, and interpret relational boundaries. We also find substantial annotator disagreement for context-dependent harmful behaviors, with disagreement varying according to annotators' political affiliation, conversation length, and the utterance's position. Together, CompanionHarm provides a foundation for detecting socio-emotional harms in multi-turn human-AI conversations and for rigorously examining how such harms are interpreted by both humans and LLMs. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/HanMeng2004/CompanionHarm.

</details>


### [275] [GenAIT: Development and Validation of an Objective Generative AI Literacy Test for High School Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25815)
*Brett Puppart,Kristjan-Julius Laak,Jaan Aru*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文报告了生成式AI素养测试（GenAIT）的开发与验证过程，这是一个包含18道多选题的测试工具，用于测量高中生在技术、实践和人类影响三个维度上的GenAI概念知识。通过对7432名爱沙尼亚高中生的调查，研究验证了该测试的心理测量学特性，支持其近似单维性、可接受的信度以及三参数逻辑斯蒂模型的良好拟合，但测量精度在低分学生中较低，因此更适合群体研究而非高风险个体分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球对高中生生成式AI素养及其评估的兴趣日益增长，但针对该人群的客观评估工具仍然不足，需要开发有效的测试工具来量化学生的GenAI概念知识。

Method: 采用迭代式开发流程，先通过专家评审验证内容效度，再对7432名爱沙尼亚高中生大规模施测，运用验证性因子分析、经典测验理论和项目反应理论评估心理测量学特征。

Result: 测试支持近似单维性，边际信度为0.72，KR-20为0.69，三参数逻辑斯蒂模型拟合良好（RMSEA=.013, TLI=.987, CFI=.990, SRMSR=.021）。测量精度对大多数学生足够但随潜在特质变化，低分学生精度较低。GenAIT得分与感知有用性、感知易用性无关，与LLM使用频率呈负相关。

Conclusion: GenAIT适用于群体层面的研究用途，而非高风险个体分类。频繁使用AI和对其持有积极态度不能作为概念理解的替代指标，这提示教育者和研究者需重视客观评估工具在AI素养评价中的必要性。

Abstract: There is growing international interest in generative AI (GenAI) literacy and its assessment among high school students, but objective assessment in this population remains underdeveloped. This article reports the iterative development and validation of the GenAI Literacy Test (GenAIT), an 18-item multiple-choice test measuring high school students' conceptual knowledge about GenAI, with content spanning technical, practical, and human-impact domains. Expert review of relevance, clarity, and comprehensiveness provided evidence of content validity. In a large-scale survey of 7432 Estonian high school students, we evaluated the psychometric functioning of the Estonian-language GenAIT using confirmatory factor analysis, classical test theory, and item response theory. Results supported approximate unidimensionality, broadly adequate reliability for group-level research (marginal reliability = .72, KR-20 = .69), and good fit of a three-parameter logistic model (RMSEA = .013, TLI = .987, CFI = .990, SRMSR = .021). Measurement precision was sufficient for the majority of students but varied substantially across the latent trait, with lower precision for lower scoring students. GenAIT is therefore more suitable for group-level research than high-stakes individual classification. GenAIT scores were unrelated to perceived usefulness and perceived ease of use, and negatively associated with LLM use frequency, suggesting that frequent use and favorable perceptions of AI should not be treated as proxies for conceptual understanding.

</details>


### [276] [Non-Great-Power Conflict and AI Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25839)
*Kristina Kempkey,Seán Boddy,Catherine Ge-Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文评估了非大国冲突（NGPC）在先进AI时代作为灾难性风险来源的重要性，推翻了"NGPC远不如大国冲突（GPC）重要"的零假设，发现NGPC可能通过多种途径（如引发大国冲突、加剧灾难性恐怖主义危害、增加先进AI失控风险）构成与GPC同等量级的风险，并识别出五个关键中间变量作为优先研究目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于先进AI与战争风险的研究几乎完全聚焦于大国冲突，理由是核武器对手之间的对抗构成最大的灾难性或生存性风险。然而，非大国冲突（包括非大国之间的战争、非大国与大国之间的战争、内战、代理人战争及涉及非国家行为体的冲突）受到的关注严重不足，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 论文构建了三个子假设框架进行系统评估：(H1) NGPC增加了大国冲突的可能性；(H2) NGPC增加了灾难性恐怖主义的预期危害；(H3) NGPC增加了先进AI系统失控的预期危害。作者通过跨途径分析，识别出贯穿这些路径的五项中间变量：信息环境质量、决策时间线压缩、大国威胁感知、能力扩散和规范侵蚀。

Result: 研究发现零假设在H1和H2上缺乏充分支持（即NGPC确实显著增加灾难性风险），而H3被识别为优先研究方向而非已定结论。论文还确认了五个反复出现的中间变量作为连接NGPC与灾难性风险的关键节点。

Conclusion: NGPC在先进AI时代的重要性被严重低估，其作为灾难性风险来源的权重可能与大GPC处于同一数量级。五个共享中间变量是最高优先级的进一步调查与干预目标，研究应超越大国中心主义视角，更全面地审视AI时代的冲突风险格局。

Abstract: Research on advanced AI and the risk of war has focused almost exclusively on great power conflict, on the grounds that confrontation between nuclear-armed adversaries poses the greatest risk of catastrophic or existential harm. Considerably less attention has been paid to non-great-power conflict (NGPC): wars between non-great powers, between non-great powers and great powers, civil wars, proxy wars, and conflicts involving nonstate actors. This paper evaluates the null hypothesis that NGPC is much less important than great power conflict (GPC) as a source of catastrophic risk in an era of increasingly capable AI, against the alternative that it is within an order of magnitude of GPC in importance. We assess three sub-hypotheses: that NGPC increases the likelihood of great power conflict; that it increases the expected harm from catastrophic terrorism; and that it increases the expected harm from loss of control over advanced AI systems. We find the null poorly supported for H1 and H2, and identify H3 as a priority for further work rather than a settled finding. We also identify five intermediate variables that recur across the pathways - information environment quality, decision-making timeline compression, great power threat perception, capability diffusion, and norm erosion - and argue that these shared nodes are the highest-priority targets for further investigation and intervention.

</details>


### [277] [Spatial-Knowledge-Graph-Grounded LLM Agents for Neighborhood Livability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25952)
*Haiyan Hao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个原型框架，结合空间知识图谱(KG)和大语言模型(LLM)及图检索增强生成(Graph-RAG)，通过生成和修订家庭日程表、规则可行性检查及GIS网络物化，来模拟不同居民在社区中的日常活动体验，评估社区宜居性的实际可达性而非仅静态设施可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统社区宜居性评估依赖静态建成环境指标（如设施邻近度、街道连通性、公共空间可达性），这些指标只描述"可用机会"，无法反映不同行动能力、家庭角色、日程安排和照护责任的居民实际体验社区的方式。需要一种能连接空间机会与个体活动的动态评估方法。

Method: 框架包含四层：1) 空间知识图谱整合居民、住所、设施、社区背景和道路节点；2) Graph-RAG检索每户家庭附近的空间上下文（POI候选和近似步行时间）供LLM生成结构化家庭日程表；3) 基于规则的轻量修复和可审计可行性检查，LLM针对识别的问题修订日程；4) 路由模块从道路网络推导实际出行路径、时间、模式和事件历史，最终支持合成居民代理访谈。

Result: 在深圳某社区的演示表明：名义上的设施可用性并不等于实际便捷可达性。行动受限的居民和承担照护责任的家庭面临更大的出行和协调负担。框架提供了可审计的方式连接空间机会、家庭活动约束和居民特定宜居性解读。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将模拟体验与观察感知区分开来，为社区宜居性评估提供了一种从静态指标走向动态、个体化、情境化评估的新路径，但其原型性质意味着仍需更多验证和扩展。

Abstract: Neighborhood livability is commonly assessed with static built-environment indicators, such as facility proximity, street connectivity, and access to public space. These measures describe available opportunities but do not directly represent how residents with different mobility capacities, household roles, schedules, and care responsibilities experience the neighborhood. This paper presents a prototype framework that uses a spatial knowledge graph (KG) and large language models (LLMs) to generate and revise household schedules, followed by rule-based feasibility checking and GIS-based network materialization. The spatial KG integrates residents, residences, facilities, neighborhood context, and sampled road hubs; Graph-RAG retrieves each household's nearby spatial context, including candidate POIs and approximate walking times, for the scheduling LLM. The LLM produces structured household schedules, while rules are used for lightweight repairs and auditable feasibility checks. The LLM then revises schedules in response to identified feasibility issues. A routing module derives the actual travel paths, travel times, modes, and event histories from the road network. The resulting events support synthetic resident-agent interviews about daily convenience, travel burden, activity feasibility, and household coordination. A prototype demonstration in a Shenzhen neighborhood shows that nominal facility availability does not necessarily imply convenient access: residents with limited mobility and households with care responsibilities experience greater travel and coordination burdens. The framework offers an auditable way to connect spatial opportunity, household activity constraints, and resident-specific livability interpretation, while keeping simulated experience distinct from observed perception.

</details>


### [278] [Giving Mechanical Engineers Intelligent Tools: A Project-Based AI Education Curriculum in Thermal Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26056)
*Changgen Li,Han Hu,Christy Dunlap,Nathaniel House,Jonathan Wai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出并介绍了阿肯色大学机械工程专业中融入人工智能（AI）的新课程体系，该课程分为初级、应用和高级三个层次，重点关注热问题及与电气和计算机工程的交叉领域，旨在提升学生对AI模型的理解、工程任务解决能力和跨学科沟通技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械工程学生面临三大挑战：跨学科知识储备不足（电气和计算机工程训练欠缺）、传统热系统建模复杂、课程负担重且课时有限。因此需要设计一种将AI融入ME课程的新方案来应对这些问题。

Method: 提出一个三级课程体系（初级、应用、高级），涵盖核心与可选AI项目，特别侧重热问题及其与电气和计算机工程的交叉应用。课程大纲、数据和代码均通过开放获取存储库公开共享。

Result: 课程的主要成果目标包括：增强学生对AI模型的理解、提高工程任务解决能力以及培养跨学科沟通能力。该课程为教育者和研究者提供了如何提升学生实践技能和创造力的课程设计参考。

Conclusion: 该课程体系为机械工程教育中融入AI提供了可复制的实践框架，通过公开开放资源促进了教育资源共享，对未来工程教育改革具有参考价值。

Abstract: Mechanical engineering (ME) requires a broad knowledge base across several disciplines. However, ME students often have insufficient training in electrical and computer engineering, complex challenges in traditional thermal system modeling, and endure heavy course loads with limited class hours. To help address these challenges, this paper proposes a new curriculum that integrates artificial intelligence (AI) into ME at the University of Arkansas (UARK), with a particular emphasis on thermal problems and their interplay with electrical and computer engineering. The curriculum has introductory, application, and advanced levels, covering core and optional AI projects. Key goals are to enhance students' understanding of AI models, ability to tackle engineering tasks, and teach multidisciplinary communication skills. This curriculum offers educators and researchers valuable insights into courses that can enhance students' practical skills and creativity. This curriculum, including the syllabus, data, and codes, is available to the public in open-access repositories.

</details>
